# 各库的 kernel 分族明细

由 `build_tables.py` 从 `inventory/*.json` 生成，不要手改。类别：torch 运算 / torch 组合（参照程序已定义）、需要定义、随机。反向 kernel 的参照程序是前向参照程序的梯度。

| 库 | 入口 kernel | 分族 | torch 运算 | torch 组合 | 需要定义 | 随机 | sm_86 不能跑的分族 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| applied-ai | 36 | 21 | 8 | 10 | 3 | 0 | 6 |
| attorch | 25 | 27 | 17 | 6 | 0 | 4 | 0 |
| DeepSpeed | 34 | 19 | 4 | 14 | 1 | 0 | 0 |
| flash-linear-attention (fla) | 466 | 147 | 2 | 58 | 87 | 0 | 21 |
| flash-attention (Triton code) | 13 | 7 | 2 | 4 | 0 | 1 | 0 |
| gemlite | 34 | 11 | 0 | 2 | 9 | 0 | 3 |
| kernl | 22 | 21 | 7 | 8 | 6 | 0 | 2 |
| lightllm | 216 | 89 | 8 | 47 | 34 | 0 | 17 |
| Lightning Attention | 16 | 4 | 1 | 1 | 2 | 0 | 0 |
| LMDeploy | 89 | 58 | 1 | 20 | 36 | 1 | 16 |
| SageAttention (Triton parts) | 9 | 7 | 0 | 1 | 6 | 0 | 0 |
| sglang | 805 | 309 | 16 | 167 | 121 | 5 | 42 |
| stk | 4 | 4 | 0 | 1 | 3 | 0 | 0 |
| trident | 52 | 48 | 37 | 8 | 0 | 3 | 0 |
| OpenAI triton_kernels (python/triton_kernels in triton-lang/triton) | 31 | 25 | 0 | 3 | 21 | 1 | 1 |

## applied-ai

https://github.com/pytorch-labs/applied-ai，提交 `2391954b1998`，许可 BSD-3-Clause (Copyright 2024 Meta); per-file headers: fused_rms_norm.py also cites Llama 2 Community License, triton_tutorial_groupgemm.py NVIDIA MIT, causal_1d_conv.py IBM；`@triton.jit` 函数 60 个，入口 kernel 36 个。

| 分族 | 方向 | 类别 | 规格类别 | 参照程序，或待定事项 | sm_86 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| flash_attn_bwd | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True, scale=d**-0.5), (q, k, v), do) | yes | Always causal (consistent with flash(causal=True), not with non-causal attention()). P and dS hardcoded .to(bfloat16) -> only bf16 inputs compile (fp16 gives dot dtype mismatch; __main__ uses fp16). Needs N_CTX % 128 == 0, seqlen_q==seqlen_k, hq==hk, identical contiguous strides; uses log2-domain LSE from fwd with k pre-scaled by sm_scale/ln2. ALiBi path calls undefined compute_alibi_block (disabled). |
| flash_attn_fwd | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=causal, scale=sm_scale)  # flash custom op: causal=True, sm_scale=d**-0.5; attention(): causal=False; aux M = torch.logsumexp(sm_scale*q@k^T, -1)/ln2 | yes | Causal mask is bottom-right aligned when seqlen_q != seqlen_k (SDPA is_causal is top-left) -> compare with equal lengths or explicit mask. Head dims padded to {32,64,128,256}; fully-masked rows -> 0 output, LSE=+inf. Constexpr paths not wired by wrappers and not covered by the reference: VARLEN cu_seqlens, additive bias, ALiBi (-slope*\|i+sk-j-sq\|), GQA with off_h_k = off_h_q % hk (differs from SDPA enable_gqa grouping), dropout (path references undefined off_hz, does not compile). |
| fp8_tma_gemm | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.mm(a.double(), b.double().T).half()  # a:[M,K], b:[N,K] float8_e4m3fn | no | Hopper-only: host fill_2d_tma_descriptor + tl._experimental_descriptor_load/store, fp8e4nv operands. Fixed 64x64x256 tiles (config argument ignored). fp32 accumulate, fp16 output. |
| rms_norm | 前向+反向 | torch 运算 |  | F.rms_norm(x, (x.shape[-1],), weight, eps=eps); bwd = autograd grads (dx, dweight) | yes | Autotuned (num_warps). fp32 compute, y cast to x.dtype; also writes rstd=1/sqrt(mean(x^2)+eps) per row (fp32). Bwd: per-SM fp32 dW partials [sm_count,N] summed in torch then cast to weight.dtype. Requires N <= 65536/elem_size. No bias, no residual. No in-repo test. A third bwd kernel exists only inside a docstring (not counted). |
| softmax_bwd | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(torch.softmax(x,-1), x, dy)  == p*(dy - (p*dy).sum(-1,keepdim=True)), p=saved softmax output | yes | DEVIATION (bug): wrapper passes (probs, grad) into the kernel's (grad_ptr, input_ptr) slots swapped, so kernel computes p*dy - dy*sum(p*dy) instead of p*dy - p*sum(p*dy). Computation in input dtype. |
| softmax_fwd | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.softmax(x, dim=-1) | yes | Two identical copies. x viewed as (-1,N), one program per row, BLOCK=next_pow2(N); max/exp/sum done in input dtype (no fp32 upcast), output dtype=input. test_softmax.py imports a non-existent module and its 3D test compares torch.softmax(dim=1) with the kernel's last-dim softmax (test bug). |
| stay_attention_persistent | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=False, scale=sm_scale)  # wrapper hardcodes sm_scale=0.5 | yes | Persistent experimental kernel with bugs: m_i/l_i not reset between output tiles (only acc reset); tile coords derived from pid instead of tile_id; k/v use q's batch/head strides; no masking (seq_len % 64 == 0, head_dim == 128). In-file test inputs randn//10 (mostly 0/-1). |
| vector_add | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | a + b | yes | Flat contiguous tensors of equal numel. Wrapper bug: a.is_cuda() called as a method -> TypeError before launch. num_elems is constexpr (recompile per size). |
| causal_conv1d_fwd | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | y = F.conv1d(torch.cat([initial_states, x], -1) if initial_states is not None else F.pad(x, (W-1, 0)), weight[:, None, :], bias, groups=dim); y = F.silu(y) if activation in ('silu','swish') else y  # x:(batch,dim,seqlen), weight:(dim,W) | yes | Autotuned. Requires channel-last memory (x.stride(1)==1), initial_states stride_dim==1. fp32 accumulation, output in x dtype. seq_idx accepted but ignored; return_final_states unsupported; autograd backward calls undefined causal_conv1d_bwd (no Triton bwd). |
| fp8_rowwise_gemm_tma_persistent | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | (torch.mm(a.double(), b.double().T) * a_scale.double()[:,None] * b_scale.double()[None,:]).to(c.dtype)  # a:[M,K], b:[N,K] fp8 e4m3fn, a_scale:[M], b_scale:[N] | no | Persistent, TMA descriptors for A/B/C and 1-D scale descriptors; fp8e4nv. Kernel multiplies by scales (scales are dequant/'reciprocal' scales: A*A_scale = original A) although matmul_fp8_row docstring says a@b/(a_scale*b_scale). prep_matmul and the non-TMA kernel are missing from the repo (non-TMA path returns None); output dtype comes from missing prep_matmul (bf16 in FBGEMM). dot_out_dtype accumulator; fp8_fast_accum/allow_tf32 unused. |
| fp8_splitk_gemm | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | (scale_a*scale_b*torch.mm(a.double(), b.double())).half()  # a:[M,K], b:[K,N]; scale_a=scale_b=1 for gemm_split_k_kernel | yes | Dtype-generic tl.dot code intended for fp8 inputs: float8_e4m3fn operands need sm89+ in Triton, so on A6000 run with fp16/bf16 (or e5m2) operands. Per-tensor python-float scales multiplied after fp32 accumulation. split_k fp32 partials tl.atomic_add'ed into zero-init fp16 C (order nondeterministic, fp16 rounding per add); 'acc.to(tl.float16)' result discarded. Loads are masked on K only: rows/cols beyond M/N read out of bounds (stores masked). |
| fused_moe_gemm | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | C.view(-1,N)[i] = (A[i//top_k].double() @ B[topk_ids.view(-1)[i]].double().T) * (topk_weights.view(-1)[i] if MUL_ROUTED_WEIGHT else 1)  for i < topk_ids.numel(); B:[E,N,K]. Full layer = test_moe_gemm.py::torch_moe: sum_j w_j*(SiluAndMul(a@w1[e_j].T)@w2[e_j].T) | yes | Precondition: sorted_token_ids/expert_ids/num_tokens_post_padded produced from topk_ids by vLLM ops.moe_align_block_size (external CUDA op); padded slots (id==numel) masked. fp16/bf16 inputs, fp32 acc, routed weight applied in fp32 before cast. v1 split-K tl.atomic_add's compute_type partials into zeroed C (fp16 fine on sm86; bf16 atomics may need sm90). v2 col_major mapping (pid_m = pid % grid_n, pid_n = pid // grid_m) is wrong unless grid_m==grid_n -> tiles skipped/duplicated. test imports a missing splitk_moe_fused module. |
| grouped_gemm_fbgemm_layout | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | for g: r in [o_g, o_g+m_sizes[g]): y[r] = (x[r].double() @ w[g*N:(g+1)*N].double().T) [* x_scale[r,None]*w_scale[None,g*N:(g+1)*N] for fp8_rowwise]; y.to(torch.bfloat16)  # x:[M,K], w:[G*N,K], y:[M,N] | no | Autotuned persistent kernels; on CUDA USE_TMA_LOAD=True (host TMA descriptors) and wrapper raises without TMA descriptor support -> Hopper only; fp8 variant uses fp8e4nv. Static assert K % BLOCK_K == 0. Rows beyond sum(m_sizes) stay uninitialised (torch.empty). |
| grouped_matmul_ptr_array | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | [torch.mm(A_i.double(), B_i.double()).half() for A_i, B_i in zip(group_A, group_B)] | yes | fp16 only (pointers cast to fp16), autotuned NUM_SM/blocks, no masking (M,N,K must be tile multiples). |
| grouped_matmul_ptr_array_tma | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | [(A_i.double() @ B_i.double().T).to(A_i.dtype) for i]  # B_i:[N,K] | no | Device tensor descriptors (tl._experimental_make_tensor_descriptor), asserts capability>=9. FP8 mode (float8_e4m3fn inputs) also writes the output as unscaled float8_e4m3fn. |
| moe_grouped_gemm_bwd | 反向 | torch 组合 |  | autograd of moe_grouped_gemm_fwd_blockdiag: grad_x[rows_g] = grad_y[rows_g, gN:(g+1)N] @ w[gN:(g+1)N]; grad_w[gN:(g+1)N] = grad_y[rows_g, gN:(g+1)N].T @ x[rows_g] | yes | USE_TMA_STORE=False so TMA code is compiled out; plain loads. Operands upcast to fp32 then tl.dot(allow_tf32=True) when K%8==0 -> TF32 rounding (deviation vs fp32 reference). group offsets computed on host via .item() loop. Exceptions swallowed with partial torch fallback. |
| moe_grouped_gemm_fwd_blockdiag | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | y[o_g:o_g+m_g, g*N:(g+1)*N] = (x[o_g:o_g+m_g].double() @ w[g*N:(g+1)*N].double().T) [* x_scale[rows,None]*w_scale[None,g*N:(g+1)*N] for fp8_rowwise]; y bf16 [M, N*G], off-diagonal blocks undefined | no | Output layout differs from FBGEMM ([M,N*G] block-diagonal, other blocks left torch.empty). USE_TMA_LOAD/STORE hardcoded True (device-side experimental_device_tensormap_create2d) -> Hopper only. Coverage bug: grid=min(NUM_SMS,4) but tiles are strided by NUM_SMS, so only tiles {0..3}+k*NUM_SMS get computed. |
| paged_attention_decode | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | K_s[t] = key_cache[block_tables[s, t//B], h, :, t%B] (same for V), t < context_lens[s]; out[s,h] = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q[s,h][None], K_s, V_s, scale=scale)[0] | yes | Single-query decode, caches [num_blocks,H,D,block_size] for both K and V (not vLLM's x-split key layout); num_kv_heads must equal num_heads (no GQA). Deviations: softmax denominator +1e-6; fp32 scratchpad round-trip. Bugs: query offset = seq_idx*num_seqs + head_idx*D (correct only for one sequence or num_seqs==H*D); v2 does not compile (weighted_values used before assignment) and never merges partitions. MAX_SEQ_LEN, HEAD_SIZE powers of 2. Debug static_print calls. |
| fp8_groupwise_quant_1x128 | 前向 | 需要定义 | F | group = 128 contiguous elements along last dim (1x128 tiles), last dim must divide by block_size；scale = max(amax, 1e-12)/448 (e4m3fn max) stored fp32 per group; dequant x ~= q*scale；q = clamp(x/scale, -448, 448) cast to float8_e4m3fn (Triton fp32->fp8 rounding mode, RNE assumed); division done in fp32；tolerance at fp8 rounding boundaries when reference divides in float64 | no | Outputs float8_e4m3fn (Triton fp8e4nv needs sm89+). Only stride(-1)==1 is checked but indexing assumes fully contiguous x (bug for other strides). Wrapper prints scales.size(). Candidate ref: s=x.view(...,-1,128).abs().amax(-1).clamp_min(1e-12)/448; q=(x/s).clamp(-448,448).to(float8_e4m3fn). |
| gptq_w4a16_dequant_gemm | 前向 | 需要定义 | G | qweight int32 [K/8,N]: 8 int4 per word packed along K, element k at bits 4*(k%8) (LSB first)；qzeros int32 [K/G,N/8] packed along N with same nibble order; stored zero is z-1 (dequant uses z+1, AutoGPTQ legacy convention)；scales [K/G,N] fp16, group = k // groupsize, groupsize hardcoded 128, no act-order/g_idx permutation；dequant w = q*s - (z+1)*s evaluated in fp16 (intermediate rounding) vs exact (q-z-1)*s；fp16 activations, fp32 accumulate, fp16 output; split-K partials atomically added in fp16 | yes | Bugs: a100/h100 (block_k=256) and small_benchmark split-K (block_k*split_k=512) compute g_id = k // (groupsize // block_k) with groupsize=128 -> integer division by zero; only splitk_dequant_gemm (block_k=128=groupsize) and matmul_data_parallel_kernel (block_k=64) index groups consistently. h100 store mask compares rows with n instead of m; no load masks anywhere (M,N,K must be tile multiples; a100/data-parallel wrap offsets with %m/%n but store unmasked). h100 fp8_fast_accum branch (tl.float, fp8e4nv) is dead (constexpr False). No in-repo correctness test. |
| gptq_w4a16_fused_moe_gemm | 前向 | 需要定义 | H | per-expert GPTQ int4 packing/zero+1/scale-group conventions (as gptq_w4a16_dequant_gemm), groupsize 128；qweight tensor shape/stride convention ([E,K/8,N] in test vs strides (0,2,1) and K=qweight.shape[2] in wrapper)；routing metadata from vLLM ops.moe_align_block_size (sorted_token_ids with sentinel=numel, expert_ids per block_m block, a-row = token//top_k)；whether topk_weights are applied (kernel never applies them) | yes | Needs vllm._C. Bugs: output stored at sorted position offs_m (not offs_token) without mask -> wrong/out-of-bounds scatter; invoke_dequant_gemm_moe overrides topk=2 so the second GEMM (needs top_k=1) reads wrong rows; K loop bound uses packed dim. Treat as unfinished prototype. |

## attorch

https://github.com/BobMcDear/attorch，提交 `67c2772baf33`，许可 MIT；`@triton.jit` 函数 82 个，入口 kernel 25 个。

| 分族 | 方向 | 类别 | 规格类别 | 参照程序，或待定事项 | sm_86 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| activation backward (21 activations, dropout=False) | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(act(x), x, grad_output) with act as in the forward family | yes | Also launched by Linear and BatchNorm backward to differentiate the fused activation w.r.t. saved pre-activation. Subgradient conventions match torch (relu'(0)=0, leaky'(0)=slope, elu'(0)=alpha, hardsigmoid/hardtanh/relu6 open intervals, hardshrink/softshrink 0 on \|x\|<=lambd). Deviations: mish_grad uses exp(x)^4 terms -> inf/inf=NaN for x>~22 in fp32 (torch ~1); tanh-based grads inherit tanh precision near 0. |
| activation forward (21 activations via constexpr act_func switch, dropout=False) | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | {'sigmoid':torch.sigmoid,'logsigmoid':F.logsigmoid,'tanh':torch.tanh,'relu':F.relu,'gelu':F.gelu(x),'geluapprox':F.gelu(x,approximate='tanh'),'silu':F.silu,'relu6':F.relu6,'hardsigmoid':F.hardsigmoid,'hardtanh':F.hardtanh(x,-1.,1.),'hardswish':F.hardswish,'selu':F.selu,'mish':F.mish,'softplus':F.softplus(x,beta=1,threshold=20),'softsign':F.softsign,'tanhshrink':F.tanhshrink,'leaky_relu':F.leaky_relu(x,param),'elu':F.elu(x,param),'celu':F.celu(x,param),'hardshrink':F.hardshrink(x,param),'softshrink':F.softshrink(x,param)}[act_func]\(x) | yes | Flat 1D elementwise over size elems; param is a runtime scalar parsed from 'name_PARAM' strings in the layer. Code-visible deviations (fp32 math): softplus=log(1+exp(x)) has no threshold -> inf for x>~88.7 (torch returns x); logsigmoid=log(sigmoid(x)) -> -inf/underflow for x<~-88 (torch ~x); tanh=2*sigmoid(2x)-1 has ~1e-7 absolute error so large relative error for \|x\|<~1e-4, tanhshrink=x-tanh(x) suffers catastrophic cancellation near 0. relu/relu6/hard*/softsign/leaky/shrink computed in input dtype; others upcast to fp32. Hardtanh layer only allows min=-1,max=1; Softplus layer has no beta/threshold args. In-place args ignored (warning). |
| conv2d forward (also AvgPool1d/2d and Conv1d) | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.conv2d(x, w, None, stride=(sh,sw), padding=(ph,pw), dilation=1, groups=groups); pools: F.avg_pool2d(x, k, stride, padding, ceil_mode=False, count_include_pad=True) | yes | Direct (implicit GEMM) conv, zero padding, dilation 1 only; bias added in Python afterwards (comment: in-kernel bias buggy with groups). TF32 on cc>=8. Pools implemented as depthwise conv with constant 1/k weights cached on first call (channel count/dtype of first input). Backward uses torch.nn.grad (no Triton). README: attorch.nn exposes torch convs instead because these are slow. |
| cross_entropy backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(F.cross_entropy(x,t,weight=w), x, g) | yes | Uses sum_weights from forward; same target-domain restriction. |
| cross_entropy forward (mean, optional class weights) | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.cross_entropy(x, t, weight=w, reduction='mean')  for 2D x [B,C], all t in [0,C) | yes | Only reduction='mean', no label smoothing, no ignore_index (module does not raise for ignore_index; target -100 -> out-of-bounds read). Writes per-program partial sums (and sum of weights) reduced in Python. BUG: output buffer has floor(B/BLOCK_SIZE_BATCH) entries but grid is cdiv; when feat_dim<64, B>=2048 and B % BLOCK_SIZE_BATCH != 0 the last program writes out of bounds and its rows are dropped (its block sum would also be NaN since masked rows load -inf). |
| flash attention backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q,k,v,is_causal=causal,scale=sm_scale), (q,k,v), do) | unknown | Recomputes P from forward L and preprocess Delta (must be the forward's outputs). dq accumulated read-modify-write into caller-zeroed buffer; sequence_parallel=True writes per-column-block dq replicas summed in Python. MMA_V3 only on cc>=9 (else transposed-dot path). BLOCK=128, num_warps=8, D up to 128 may exceed sm86 smem. Deprecated. |
| flash attention forward (Triton tutorial v2 copy) | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=causal, scale=sm_scale) for q,k,v [Z,H,N,D]; aux L = log2-domain logsumexp: (sm_scale*log2(e)*q@k^T).logsumexp-in-base-2 per row (m + log2 l) | unknown | DEPRECATED: MultiheadAttention.__init__ raises 'currently deprecated'; layer also hard-codes sm_scale=0.5 (not 1/sqrt(D)) and passes non-contiguous transposed q/k/v while kernel computes offsets as off_hz*stride_qh//stride_qm (assumes contiguous [Z,H,N,D]). No bounds checks: N must be a multiple of 128, D in {16,32,64,128}, q and kv same length. q pre-scaled and cast to input dtype before dot. Requires cc>=8 (raises otherwise); BLOCK 128x64, num_stages=4 may exceed sm86 99KB smem for D=128. |
| layer_norm backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(F.layer_norm(x,(D,),w,b,eps), (x,w,b), g) | yes | Consumes saved mean/rstd. dW/db written as per-program partial sums of shape [cdiv(B,BLOCK_SIZE_BATCH), D], summed in Python; check after sum. |
| layer_norm forward | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.layer_norm(x, (D,), weight, bias, eps)  (+ optional stats: mean=x.mean(-1), rstd=1/sqrt(x.var(-1,unbiased=False)+eps)) | yes | Normalizes last dim only (normalized_shape must be int). bias is ignored when weight is None (add_bias requires scale_by_weight) - differs from F.layer_norm(x,(D,),None,b). Output fp32 under AMP unless autocast_to_fp32=False (documented). Whole row per block. |
| nll_loss backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(F.nll_loss(x,t,weight=w,reduction=r), x, g) | yes | Scatters into a zero-initialized input_grad (wrapper zeros it). |
| nll_loss forward (none/mean/sum, optional class weights, arbitrary spatial dims) | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.nll_loss(x, t, weight=w, reduction=reduction)  with all t in [0,C) | yes | ignore_index unsupported (documented, silently ignored). Spatial dims flattened and processed in one block (next_pow2(spatial_dim)). Same floor-vs-cdiv partial-sum bug as cross_entropy for reduction!='none' when spatial_dim<64, B>=2048, B % BLOCK_SIZE_BATCH != 0. |
| pointwise regression loss backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(loss_ref(x,t), (x,t), g) | yes | Deviation: L1 (and smooth L1 with beta=0) grad at diff==0 is +1 (where(0<=diff,1,-1)); torch uses sign -> 0. Also writes target grad = -input grad. |
| pointwise regression loss forward (smooth L1 / L1 / MSE / Huber via constexpr p_loss) | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | {0: F.smooth_l1_loss(x,t,reduction=r,beta=param), 1: F.l1_loss(x,t,reduction=r), 2: F.mse_loss(x,t,reduction=r), 3: F.huber_loss(x,t,reduction=r,delta=param)}[p_loss] | yes | Flattened; per-program partial sums reduced in Python using autotuner best_config BLOCK_SIZE. No broadcasting between input and target. |
| rms_norm backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(F.rms_norm(x,(D,),w,eps), (x,w), g) | yes | dW partial sums per program, summed in Python. |
| rms_norm forward | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.rms_norm(x, (D,), weight, eps)  with eps=None -> torch.finfo(x.dtype).eps (same default as torch) | yes | Optionally saves inv_rms. Last dim only. |
| softmax / log_softmax / softmin backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(forward_ref(x), x, g) | yes | Takes the saved forward output (not x) as input; reference should feed y=forward_ref(x). |
| softmax / log_softmax / softmin forward (last dim) | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | neg=False,log=False: torch.softmax(x,-1); log=True: F.log_softmax(x,-1); neg=True: F.softmin(x,-1); neg&log: F.log_softmax(-x,-1) | yes | 2D [batch, feat] view, whole row in one block (BLOCK_SIZE_FEAT=next_pow2(feat_dim)) so very wide rows may fail to compile/spill. Fully -inf row -> NaN (same as torch). fp32 math; output fp32 under autocast else input dtype. Layers reject dim != last. |
| GLU backward | 反向 | torch 组合 |  | torch.autograd.grad(a*act(b), (a, b), g) | yes | Writes separate grads for both halves; layer concatenates along dim. |
| GLU forward with arbitrary activation | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | a, b = x.chunk(2, dim); a * act(b)   (act='sigmoid' == F.glu(x, dim)) | yes | Kernel receives the two halves as separate contiguous buffers (first half = value, second half = gate, matching torch.glu). act/param as in activation family, no dropout; inherits activation numerical deviations. |
| batch_norm backward | 反向 | torch 组合 |  | torch.autograd.grad(F.batch_norm(x,None,None,w,b,training=True,eps=eps), (x,w,b), pre_act_grad) | yes | Activation grad computed first by act_func_backward_kernel; residual grad = pre_act_grad. Formula is batch-statistics gradient only. Deviation: in eval mode with track_running_stats, forward never writes mean/inv_std (left torch.empty) and backward still applies batch-stat formula -> garbage/wrong grads (correct would be w*rstd_running*g). |
| batch_norm forward with residual add and fused activation | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | act(F.batch_norm(x, running_mean, running_var, weight, bias, training=(is_train or not track_running_stats), momentum=momentum, eps=eps) + residual)  (running stats updated in place with unbiased var when is_train and track_running_stats) | yes | One program per channel, streaming Welford merge (verified algebraically correct); input viewed [N,C,S] and transposed to [S,C,N] when S>1. Deviations: momentum=None (cumulative average) unsupported; num_batches_tracked not incremented; affine requires both weight and bias; N*S==1 in training -> div by zero in n/(n-1) (torch raises). Masked loads have no `other` and tl.sum(curr_input) relies on zero-fill. Whole first-axis block loaded at once (BLOCK_SIZE_BATCH=next_pow2). Saves pre-activation for backward. autotune restores running stats between trials. |
| flash attention backward preprocess (delta) | 反向 | torch 组合 |  | (o.float() * do.float()).sum(-1) | yes | Assumes contiguous [Z*H*N, D] and N multiple of 128; unmasked loads. Deprecated with the attention module. |
| linear forward with bias and fused activation | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | act(x @ W + b) == act(F.linear(x, W.T, b)), W stored [in_features, out_features] | yes | Weight layout is transposed vs torch (documented). tf32 heuristic = cc>=8, so fp32 inputs use TF32 dot on A6000 (~1e-3 rel error); fp16 path when autocast. Pre-activation saved when grad needed. K-dim masked loads without other rely on zero-fill. Linear backward matmuls run in torch (no Triton kernel). |
| activation backward with fused dropout (dropout=True) | 反向 | 随机 | R | torch.where(m, 0, g/(1-p)) * act'(x), m regenerated from same (seed, offset) | yes | Deterministic check: mask must coincide with forward mask for the same seed; given mask, gradient is exact autograd of forward. |
| activation forward with fused dropout (dropout=True) | 前向 | 随机 | R | torch.where(m, 0, act(x)/(1-p)) with m = tl.rand(seed, flat_offset) < p | yes | Same kernel, constexpr dropout=True (training and drop_p>0). Deterministic check: conditional on mask m (recover from zeros where act(x)!=0, or regenerate Philox tl.rand(seed, element index), independent of BLOCK_SIZE) the kept values must equal act(x)/(1-p); dropped fraction ~p. Seed = python randint(0,65535). |
| dropout backward | 反向 | 随机 | R | torch.where(m, 0, g/(1-p)) with the forward's mask | yes | Mask regenerated from (seed, offset); check equality with forward mask. |
| dropout forward | 前向 | 随机 | R | torch.where(m, 0, x/(1-p)), m = tl.rand(seed, flat_offset) < p | yes | Kernel only launched for training and 0<p<1 (p==0/eval returns input, p==1 returns zeros in Python). Deterministic check: kept elems == x/(1-p) exactly up to rounding; zero fraction ~p; same seed reproduces same mask. |

## DeepSpeed

https://github.com/deepspeedai/DeepSpeed，提交 `5f266e26d216`，许可 Apache-2.0 (deepspeed/moe/ep_kernels.py: Apache-2.0 AND BSD-3-Clause, TorchTitan-derived)；`@triton.jit` 函数 35 个，入口 kernel 34 个。

| 分族 | 方向 | 类别 | 规格类别 | 参照程序，或待定事项 | sm_86 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| chunked_cross_entropy (per-row CE with ignore_index) | 前向+反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(logits.double(), target, ignore_index=ignore_index, reduction='none'); grad = autograd (ignored rows -> zero grad) | yes | online log-sum-exp in fp32, loss fp32 per row; backward writes grad in logits dtype. domain: targets in [0,V) or == ignore_index (no bounds check; out-of-range target reads OOB where torch raises). |
| flash_attention_fwd (diffusers inference, non-causal, fp16) | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q.double(), k.double(), v.double(), scale=sm_scale).half() | yes | q,k,v,o [Z,H,N,D]; make_block_ptr without boundary_check (plain loads on sm86). domain: fp16, N_CTX multiple of BLOCK (128 or 64), D pow2; all four tensors must share strides and be contiguous over (Z,H) because only off_hz*stride_qh is used as base offset. deviation: q pre-scaled by sm_scale*log2e and rounded to fp16; P cast to fp16 before PV dot. |
| gelu_erf | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.nn.functional.gelu(x.double(), approximate='none').to(x.dtype) | yes | helper gelu_functor (x*0.5*(1+erf(x/1.41421356237))) is also used by _fp_matmul epilogue. domain: contiguous input, flat 1D grid. deviation: 1/sqrt(2) truncated constant; x/c is promoted to fp32 (Triton div rule) but 0.5*x stays in input dtype for fp16. |
| rms_norm (HF Llama-style, cast before gamma) | 前向+反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.nn.functional.rms_norm(x.double(), (N,), weight.double(), eps).to(x.dtype); grads = autograd of the same | yes | forward stores fp32 rstd; dweight via per-row-block fp32 partials then torch sum. deviation (intended, mirrors eager): normalised value rounded to input dtype before *weight; backward rounds grad_out*weight and the dweight product to input dtype. domain: bf16/fp16 only, N<=2048 (one block per row), eps is constexpr (recompiles per eps). |
| autoep_invert_index (sorted-row permutation inverse) | — | torch 组合 |  | inv = torch.empty(n, dtype=torch.int32); inv[index.long()] = torch.arange(index.numel(), dtype=torch.int32)  (== torch.argsort(index).int() for a permutation) | yes | domain: index must be a permutation of [0,n); out-of-range targets are skipped, uncovered entries stay uninitialised (torch.empty), duplicate targets race (nondeterministic winner). |
| autoep_row_weighting (per-row fp32 routing weight) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | (rows.double() * weights.double()).to(rows.dtype)  with weights [N,1]; grads = autograd | yes | domain: rows [N,H] contiguous bf16/fp16, weights [N,1] contiguous fp32. grad_weights reduced via per-hidden-block fp32 partials then a second kernel. |
| autoep_weighted_restore (top-k combine of expert rows) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | buf = torch.zeros(T*K, H, dtype=torch.float64); buf[token_indices_sorted] = rows.double(); (buf.view(T,K,H) * top_scores.double().view(T,K,1)).sum(1).to(rows.dtype); grads = autograd of the same | yes | forward sums the K slots in slot order in fp32 with enable_fp_fusion=False; backward scatters grad rows through the inverse index and reduces grad_scores in fp32. domain: token_indices_sorted is a full permutation of [0,T*K) (else inverse has garbage entries); rows fp16/bf16/fp32; score_apply must be 'post'. |
| ep_fill_permute_indices (expert-major padded index fill) | — | torch 组合 |  | candidate is the lib's loop: deepspeed/moe/ep_kernels.py::fill_indices_cpu (out=full(max_len,-1); for e: w=write_offsets[e]; for r: i=r*E+e; out[w:w+len_i]=arange(start_i,start_i+len_i); w+=len_i) | yes | all inputs explicit, output fixed by the API; integer exact. deviation: kernel does not clamp writes at max_len (CPU reference does) -> OOB write if write_offsets+counts exceed max_len; -1 fill comes from the wrapper. |
| ep_gather_rows (permute with zero padding rows) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | N=src.shape[0]; torch.cat([src, src.new_zeros(1, H)])[torch.where((index>=0)&(index<N), index, N).long()] | yes | pure copy, exact. Used as forward of permute_rows and backward of unpermute_rows (backward of permute uses torch index_select). |
| grouped_gemm (2D x 3D, torch._grouped_mm semantics) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | starts=[0]+offs[:-1]; torch.cat([a[s:e].double() @ (b[g].double().T if trans_b else b[g].double()) for g,(s,e) in enumerate(zip(starts, offs))]); dw: b_grad[g] = a[s:e].T @ dout[s:e] | yes | group of each M-tile found by an unrolled scan over NUM_GROUPS (constexpr); grid upper bound cdiv(M,BM)+E. domain: offs int32 cumulative row ends, non-decreasing, offs[-1]==M (rows past offs[-1] stay uninitialised), empty groups allowed, mat_b contiguous, same dtype fp16/bf16/fp32. deviation: fp32 inputs use TF32 dot (documented). |
| grouped_gemm_group_meta (offs -> start/size) | — | torch 组合 |  | m_start = torch.cat([offs.new_zeros(1), offs[:-1]]).int(); m_size = (offs - m_start).int() | yes | integer bookkeeping, exact. |
| inference_gemm (2D gemm+bias+activation; batched 4D gemm incl. packed-QKV score/context) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | 2D: act(A.double()@B.double() + bias) with act in {none, relu, leaky_relu(0.01), gelu(erf), sigmoid}; 4D: (a.double()@b.double()) * (scale if scale>0 else 1); score: q@k^T*scale with q,k = qkv[...,:D],qkv[...,D:2D] viewed [B,S,H,hd]->[B,H,S,hd]; context: (prob@v) written back as [B,S,H*hd] | yes | _fp_matmul: autotuned (all configs SPLIT_K=1; SPLIT_K>1 path would use atomic_add), EVEN_K heuristic, ACC fp32 (int32 for non-float inputs), bias+activation applied in fp32 before a single cast. matmul_4d_kernel: masked loads; MASK=True (causal -inf, top-left aligned) path exists but every wrapper passes MASK=False. deviation: fp32 inputs use TF32 dot; 4D kernel rounds the product to the output dtype before multiplying by scale (double rounding); MASK early-exit path stores -inf tiles without bounds mask (OOB write if ever enabled); wrapper _score_4d_matmul ignores its input_mask/triangular arg, so the non-flash triangular attention path applies no causal mask. |
| layer_norm (optional fused residual / input-bias pre-add) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | torch.nn.functional.layer_norm((a + residual + input_bias).double(), (N,), weight.double(), bias.double(), eps).to(a.dtype)  (residual/input_bias terms only for the fused variants) | yes | two-pass mean/var in fp32 per row. deviation: fused variants store a+res(+b) into a scratch ln_input of the input dtype; the mean uses the unrounded fp32 sum but variance and normalisation re-read the rounded copy; ln_input is not returned. domain: contiguous tensors, last-dim normalisation, BLOCK capped at 8192 with looping. |
| packed_qkv_flash_attention (inference, fp16) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | q,k,v = qkv.double().view(B,S,3,H,hd).permute(2,0,3,1,4); torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q,k,v, attn_mask=(mask.view(B,1,1,S) if add_mask else None), is_causal=causal, scale=sm_scale).transpose(1,2).reshape(B,S,H*hd).half() | yes | layout fixed by API: last dim = [Q\|K\|V], each H heads of hd contiguous; output fp16 [B,S,H*hd]; mask is an additive key-padding mask shared by all heads/queries; causal and mask never combined (add_mask = not triangular). domain: fp16 only, hd in pow2 16..128, mask indexed with mask.stride(1) as the batch stride (works for contiguous [B,1,1,S]/[B,1,S], wrong for [B,S]). deviation: QK^T accumulated in fp16 (out_dtype=tl.float16) and q pre-scaled by sm_scale*log2(e) then rounded to fp16; additive mask added in the log2 domain without the log2(e) factor (effective mask*ln2); out-of-range keys get -10000 not -inf; mask loads are unmasked past S (OOB read). |
| residual_add_bias (inference transformer residual) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | mlp_after_attn&pre_attn_norm: h+(r+fb+a+ab)/mp; mlp_after_attn&!pre_attn_norm: h+r+fb; else: h+a+(r+fb)/mp+(ab/mp if add_attn_bias else 0)  (all .double(), biases broadcast over last dim) | yes | domain: hidden/residual/attn_output same shape [B,S,H] and contiguous (not asserted; bias indexed as offset % H), all same dtype; computed in input dtype (no fp32 upcast). mp_size is constexpr. |
| rotary_pos_emb (HF split-half) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | c,s = cos.unsqueeze(1).double(), sin.unsqueeze(1).double(); x*c + torch.cat((-x[...,D//2:], x[...,:D//2]), -1)*s  (for q and k; BACKWARD=True is the transpose: g*c + rot_half^T(g*s)) | yes | rounds each product to input dtype as eager does, launched with enable_fp_fusion=False. domain: q,k 4D [B,H,S,D] (or [B,S,H,D] via unsqueeze_dim=2), cos/sin [B or 1,S,D] same dtype bf16/fp16, D even <=512, unit-stride last dim, cos/sin constant. |
| softmax_lastdim (optional additive mask) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | torch.softmax(x.double() + (mask.double() if mask is not None else 0), dim=-1).to(x.dtype) | yes | one row per program, whole row in one block (BLOCK=next_pow2(n_cols)), fp32 math. domain: contiguous input; mask viewed as [-1,n_cols] and either a single row (stride 0) or exactly one row per flattened input row; other broadcast shapes (e.g. [B,1,1,S] vs [B,H,S,S]) read out of bounds. deviation: masked kernel subtracts the max before adding the mask (same math, different overflow behaviour). |
| swiglu (separate and packed gate/up) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | torch.nn.functional.silu(gate.double()) * up.double(); packed: gate, up = gate_up.chunk(2, dim=-1); backward = autograd of the same | yes | fp32 internal math, single rounding to input dtype; int64 offsets. domain: gate/up same shape+dtype (fp16/bf16/fp32), packed last dim even; packed backward refuses create_graph. |
| fp8_weight_only_gemm (DeepSpeed FP_Quantize 8-bit weights x bf16/fp16 activations) | 前向 | 需要定义 | G | 8-bit code decoding: kernel uses a bit trick (E4M3-style, exponent rebias +120 for bf16 / +8 for fp16) with no zero/subnormal/NaN handling, so code 0x00 decodes to 2^-7*scale; must be reconciled with the CUDA dequantizer；scale granularity/layout: one fp32 scale per quantization_group_size consecutive elements of the row-major [K,N] weight, indexed per N-tile (assumes group_size >= BLOCK_N and tile-aligned)；quantiser rounding (q_range clipping, optional stochastic rounding) that produced the codes；output rounding: dequantised weight rounded to bf16/fp16 before tl.dot, fp32 accumulate, single cast | yes | no fp8 dtypes or dot_scaled: uint8 bit manipulation + bf16/fp16 tl.dot, so runs on sm86. domain: weight uint8 [K,N] contiguous, offs %M/%N wrap, K multiple of BLOCK_K (bf16 kernel's weight mask ignores the k offset; scale loads unmasked), group_size>=BLOCK_N (64, or 128 when M>=256). deviation: fp16 kernel shifts uint8 values before widening ((w&0x80)<<8, (w&0x7f)<<7); under current Triton scalar promotion the scalar does not widen uint8, so sign/exponent bits are lost; only the bf16 path is tested. |

## flash-linear-attention (fla)

https://github.com/fla-org/flash-linear-attention，提交 `15396e604df6`，许可 MIT；`@triton.jit` 函数 537 个，入口 kernel 466 个。

| 分族 | 方向 | 类别 | 规格类别 | 参照程序，或待定事项 | sm_86 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| logcumsumexp along time (used by ABC chunk) | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.logcumsumexp(s, dim=1) for s laid out [B*H, T, S] | yes | Suspected deviation: in the last partial block, padded rows are loaded as 0.0 (not -inf) and enter the running max, so if valid values are << 0 the exp underflows and the result is clamped to log(1e-20)+max; any within-block range > ~88 also hits the 1e-20 clamp. Launched from fla/ops/abc/chunk.py. |
| softmax over last dim fwd+bwd | 前向+反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.softmax(x, dim=-1); bwd: autograd (ds = p * (dp - (p * dp).sum(-1, keepdim=True))) | yes | Output dtype fp32 by default; whole row in one block (D up to next_pow2). |
| AttnRes backward (Triton) | 反向 | torch 组合 |  | torch.autograd.grad of the AttnRes reference w.r.t. (query, residuals, rms_weight, output_rms_weight) | yes | checkpoint_level=1 recomputes the mixed residual from sources; dq/dw reduced over N in a second kernel. p is marked non-differentiable (no gradient through returned weights). |
| AttnRes depth-softmax residual aggregation forward (Triton) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | V=torch.stack([r.reshape(-1,D) for r in residuals]); Kn=F.rms_norm(V,(D,),rms_weight,rms_eps); p=torch.softmax(torch.einsum('lnd,d->ln',Kn,query.flatten()*scale),dim=0); o=torch.einsum('ln,lnd->nd',p,V); if output_rms_weight is not None: o=F.rms_norm(o,(D,),output_rms_weight,rms_eps); return o.view(residuals[0].shape) (+ p.view(L,*shape[:-1]) if return_weights) | yes | Softmax over the L residual sources per row; logit = (v.(q*w))*rstd = rms_norm(v)*w . q. Defaults eps=1e-6, scale=1.0. Output (and saved o_pre) stored in residual dtype; probs returned only if return_weights (non-differentiable). Residual pointers passed as a tuple padded to L2=max(8,next_pow2(L)). |
| AttnRes forward+backward (Gluon backend) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | V=torch.stack([r.reshape(-1,D) for r in residuals]); Kn=F.rms_norm(V,(D,),rms_weight,rms_eps); p=torch.softmax(torch.einsum('lnd,d->ln',Kn,query.flatten()*scale),dim=0); o=torch.einsum('ln,lnd->nd',p,V); if output_rms_weight is not None: o=F.rms_norm(o,(D,),output_rms_weight,rms_eps); return o.view(residuals[0].shape) (+ p.view(L,*shape[:-1]) if return_weights) ; backward = autograd of this | yes | Opt-in (FLA_ATTNRES_GLUON=1 or FLA_GLUON=1), needs Triton>=3.5 gluon; verifier requires CUDA SM80+ (uses Ampere cp.async) and D*itemsize>=128 bytes, otherwise falls back to the Triton kernels. |
| Comba chunk-local cumsum (inclusive g1, exclusive g0) fwd/bwd | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | fwd: gs=g*scale; g1=cumsum(gs) within BT-chunks along T (restart at chunk/cu_seqlens boundaries); g0=g1-gs. bwd: dg[t]=sum_{s>t, same chunk} dg0[s] (VJP of the unscaled exclusive cumsum) | yes | bwd ignores the fwd scale (=1/ln2 in chunk_comba) on purpose: gradient is w.r.t. natural-log g. chunk_size must be a power of 2. |
| CyFA clock cumsum | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | clock = delta.float().cumsum(dim=1) restarted at every cu_seqlens segment (initial lambda0 added by the caller) | yes | fp32 sequential-block accumulation (BT=256). Precision note: phases use floor(lambda) mod (M-1), so fp32 clock rounding at large lambda changes phases versus a float64 reference; expect large discrepancies on long sequences. |
| CyFA fused Q/K RMSNorm (forward + backward) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | q_out=F.rms_norm(q.float(),(D,),q_weight.float(),q_eps).to(q.dtype); k_out=F.rms_norm(k.float(),(D,),k_weight.float(),k_eps).to(k.dtype); rstd=torch.rsqrt(x.float().pow(2).mean(-1)+eps); backward = autograd | yes | Shared by chunk and fused_recurrent CyFA paths; outputs rounded to input dtype like the naive _rmsnorm_forward. |
| GDN gate (-exp(A_log)*softplus(g+dt_bias)) fwd, fused chunk-local cumsum fwd, bwd | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | y=-torch.exp(A_log)*F.softplus(g+dt_bias) (dt_bias optional, per-head over last dim; output_dtype default fp32); chunk variant: o=scale*cumsum of y within BT-chunks along T (restart at chunk and cu_seqlens boundaries; REVERSE=suffix sum); bwd: torch.autograd.grad of y w.r.t. (g, A_log, dt_bias) | yes | softplus on NVIDIA is inline PTX ex2/lg2.approx.ftz of log(1+exp(x)) with threshold 20 (F.softplus default threshold matches) - loses accuracy for x<<0 (no log1p) and flushes denormals. Chunk variant called with scale=1/ln2 by chunk GDN. |
| KDA gate (fused gate fwd/bwd, gate + chunk-local cumsum) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | y = -torch.exp(A_log).view(H,1) * F.softplus(g + dt_bias.view(H,K)); if lower_bound is not None: y = lower_bound * torch.sigmoid((torch.exp(A_log).view(H,1) if A_log is not None else 1.) * (g + dt_bias.view(H,K))); chunk-cumsum kernel: out = scale * cumsum of y over T within blocks of chunk_size, blocks restarting at each sequence start (cu_seqlens) | yes | softplus threshold 20 equals torch default; NVIDIA path uses ex2/lg2.approx PTX. HAS_BETA (sigmoid beta) branch never used by wrappers. dA_log/dbias reduced from per-block partials. Gate switches to sigmoid form whenever lower_bound is set (independent of safe_gate). Also used by GDN2 and precond-KDA; chunk-cumsum backward = reverse cumsum + kda_gate_bwd. |
| LightNet gate (cumulative key normalisation, fwd+bwd) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | z = torch.logaddexp(torch.logcumsumexp(x.double(), dim=1), z0 if z0 is not None else -inf); zp = torch.cat([z0 or full(-inf), z[:, :-1]], 1); k = torch.exp(x - z); g = torch.where(zp == -inf, 0, zp - z); final = z[:, -1:]  (applied per segment when cu_seqlens given; empty segment keeps z0 or -inf) | yes | Docstring pins shapes ([B,T,H,K] logits, fp32 log-normaliser [N,1,H,K]), zero first gate for empty prefix, empty-sequence behaviour. Result feeds chunk_gla/fused_recurrent_gla (chunk_lightnet/fused_recurrent_lightnet). Uses tl.gather and tl.associative_scan (needs a Triton with tl.gather). Gradients for x and initial_state. |
| Parallax cached decode / prefill (multi-query and single-token) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | Per sequence: K,V repeat_interleave(HQ//H) over heads; S1=scale*Q@K^T masked causal (and i-j<W); P=softmax(S1,-1); S2=R@K^T (unscaled); o=(P@V)*(1+(P*S2).sum(-1,keepdim=True))-(P*S2)@V with query row i at absolute position Skv-Sq+i, keys restricted to [cache_start[b], Skv) and the window; rows with no visible key output 0 | yes | Forward-only inference kernels; left-padding via cache_start; one_step requires Sq==1. |
| Parallax parallel backward | 反向 | torch 组合 |  | torch.autograd.grad of the Parallax reference w.r.t. (q,r,k,v) | yes | dK/dV computed per q-head then folded to KV heads; uses saved m, d1, bart. |
| Parallax parallel forward | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | Per sequence: K,V repeat_interleave(HQ//H) over heads; S1=scale*Q@K^T masked causal (and i-j<W); P=softmax(S1,-1); S2=R@K^T (unscaled); o=(P@V)*(1+(P*S2).sum(-1,keepdim=True))-(P*S2)@V | yes | Docstring pins: scale only on q.k, r un-scaled, window keeps W keys incl. diagonal, GQA by divisibility, varlen B==1. Wrapper rejects non-bf16/fp16 inputs (compare float64 reference on the low-precision inputs). Also emits barv=O1/d1, d1, bart=d2/d1, m for backward. Tile BT=64 on non-Hopper. |
| RWKV7 channel mixing (token-shift mix, relu^2) fwd+bwd | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | xx = torch.cat((x_prev.view(B,1,D), x[:, :-1]), 1) - x; out = torch.relu((x + xx * x_k) @ key_weight).pow(2) @ value_weight | yes | Suspected bug: returned state x[-1, :] is the last batch element ([T,D]), not the last token x[:, -1] (same in torch ref). Backward with inplace=True (default) writes dx/dx_prev into the saved x/x_prev buffers. Matmuls done in torch; Triton covers mix, relu^2 and their grads. |
| RWKV7 fused addcmul (token-shift lerp for r,w,k,v,a[,g]) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | tuple(torch.addcmul(hidden_states, delta, m) for m in (xr, xw, xk, xv, xa) + ((xg,) if xg is not None else ())) | yes | Mix params [1,1,D]. Forward fma in input dtype (no fp32 upcast); backward accumulates in fp16 for fp16 inputs else fp32; backward (inplace default) overwrites saved hidden_states with its grad; T==1 uses torch path. |
| RWKV7 fused k update | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | k * (1 + (a - 1) * ka) | yes | Elementwise, cu_seqlens irrelevant to semantics; long path with varlen launches a grid dim of N so each chunk is recomputed N times (identical writes). dka reduced over (B,T). T==1 uses torch ref. |
| RWKV7 gate output correction | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | (o + ((r * k * r_k).sum(-1, keepdim=True) * v).reshape(o.shape)) * g | yes | Backward kernel addresses k and v with r's strides (safe only because input_guard makes same-shape contiguous tensors). T processed in 65536 slices. |
| Wall attention parallel backward | 反向 | torch 组合 |  | torch.autograd.grad of the Wall reference w.r.t. (q,k,v,g,g_scalar,sink_bias) | yes | Env WALL_ATTN_DKV_DIAG_BF16=1 (default) does the dkv diagonal block in bf16 (precision deviation); dg via reverse cumsum. |
| Wall attention parallel forward (per-channel decay inside softmax logits) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | Gc=g.cumsum(1) per sequence (natural-log per-channel decay, [B,T,HQ,K]); S[b,h,i,j]=scale*sum_n q[b,i,h,n]*k[b,j,h//G,n]*exp(Gc[b,i,h,n]-Gc[b,j,h,n]) (+ Cs[b,i,h]-Cs[b,j,h], Cs=g_scalar.cumsum(1), unscaled); mask j<=i, i-j<W, same segment; P=softmax(S) with optional denominator-only sink logit; o=P@V | yes | Input domain: g<=0 assumed (off-diagonal rescale relies on exp2<=1); diagonal sub-block clamps the key rescale exponent at 110 (log2), so huge \|g\| within a sub-block deviates; rescaled q/k cast to input dtype before dot in off-diagonal blocks. naive.py computes in fp32. |
| [Ascend] AttnRes forward+backward | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | V=torch.stack([r.reshape(-1,D) for r in residuals]); Kn=F.rms_norm(V,(D,),rms_weight,rms_eps); p=torch.softmax(torch.einsum('lnd,d->ln',Kn,query.flatten()*scale),dim=0); o=torch.einsum('ln,lnd->nd',p,V); if output_rms_weight is not None: o=F.rms_norm(o,(D,),output_rms_weight,rms_eps); return o.view(residuals[0].shape) (+ p.view(L,*shape[:-1]) if return_weights) ; backward = autograd of this | no | Triton-Ascend NPU backend (dispatched only when IS_NPU); chunks L and merges online-softmax partials in torch; also accepts residuals pre-stacked as one 4-D tensor. |
| [Ascend] GDN gate fwd / chunk-cumsum / bwd | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | same as CUDA GDN gate: -exp(A_log)*F.softplus(g+dt_bias), optional scaled chunk-local cumsum, autograd bwd | no | NPU-only dispatch (TritonAscendGDNBackend, priority 0); uses non-PTX softplus. |
| [Ascend] KDA gate | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | same as 'KDA gate' family | no | Triton-Ascend (Huawei NPU) backend: dispatched only when IS_NPU; tuned for NPU UB/core grid. Same formulas as CUDA kernel (checked). |
| [Ascend] activations (sigmoid, logsigmoid, swish, SwiGLU, PowGLU) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | same as the CUDA activation families (torch.sigmoid, F.logsigmoid/T, F.silu, F.silu(x)*y, PowGLU formula); bwd = autograd | no | Triton-Ascend backend, dispatched only when IS_NPU (Huawei Ascend). |
| [Ascend] causal_conv1d (fwd, bwd, dh0, final states, decode update) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | same as CUDA causal_conv1d forward / backward / states+update families | no | NPU-only dispatch; uses Ascend-only builtins (tl.extract_slice/insert_slice / extra.cann). |
| [Ascend] chunk_scaled_dot_kkt forward (common) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | same as CUDA chunk_scaled_dot_kkt: A[b,t,h,j]=beta*(k_t·k_s)*2**(g_t-g_s) for s=chunk_start(t)+j<t else 0 | no | NPU-only dispatch; uses Ascend-only slice builtins. |
| [Ascend] fused losses (cross-entropy, linear cross-entropy, KL div, GRPO) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | same as the CUDA fused cross-entropy / linear cross-entropy / KL / GRPO families | no | NPU-only dispatch. elementwise_mul_kernel exists in both the FLCE and KL Ascend files. Ascend FLCE fuses CE fwd+grad in cross_entropy_kernel; multi-rank process groups unsupported on Ascend. |
| [Ascend] norms (layer/rms norm, fused norm-gate, l2norm) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | same as CUDA layer_norm/rms_norm(+residual), fused norm-gate, and l2norm (x*rsqrt(sum x^2 + eps)) families | no | NPU-only dispatch (registered for layer_norm_fwd/bwd, layer_norm_gated_fwd/bwd, l2norm_fwd/bwd). |
| [Ascend] ops/utils helpers (chunk local/global cumsum, solve_tril, fused beta sigmoid) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | same as CUDA chunk cumsum, solve_tril ((I+tril(A,-1))^-1 per chunk) and fused beta sigmoid families | no | NPU-only dispatch via the triton_ascend backend registry. |
| [Ascend] rotary embedding | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | same as CUDA rotary family (rotary_embedding_ref) | no | NPU-only dispatch. |
| activations: gated linear units (SwiGLU, SigmoidGLU, PowGLU) fwd + fused fwd/bwd | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | swiglu: F.silu(x) * y; sigmoidglu: torch.sigmoid(x) * y; powglu: torch.where(x > 0, x.clamp_min(tiny) ** (power / (x.clamp_min(tiny).sqrt() + 1)) * torch.sigmoid(x), x * torch.sigmoid(x)) * y (power default 3.0); fwdbwd kernels = autograd (dx, dy) of these, optionally re-emitting z for the *_linear variants (F.linear done in torch) | yes | PowGLU gate formula fixed by docstring: x^(p/(sqrt(x)+1))*sigmoid(x) for x>0 else swish; kernel feeds only x>0 lanes to log/sqrt. *_linear variants (SwiGLULinear etc.) fuse only the activation; the projection is F.linear in torch. |
| activations: unary elementwise (sigmoid, elu+1, logsigmoid/temperature, swish) fwd+bwd | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | sigmoid: torch.sigmoid(x); elu_p1: F.elu(x) + 1; logsigmoid: F.logsigmoid(x) / temperature; swish: F.silu(x); bwd = autograd of each | yes | Rows of any stride (row-strided [*, D] views); fp32 math, output in input dtype. temperature default 1.0. Pure elementwise; bwd kernels recompute from x. |
| block-selection to CSR inversion (NSA backward helper) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | valid = (slot < block_counts) & (0 <= idx < num_blocks) & (idx*block_size <= t); block_id = (b*H + h)*num_blocks + idx (dense) or (chunk_offsets[seq(t)] + idx)*H + h (varlen); csr_offsets = cat([0], torch.bincount(block_id[valid], minlength=NB).cumsum(0)); csr_indices[:nnz] = (b*T + t)[valid] grouped by block_id (e.g. stable argsort) | yes | Integer op. Order of query indices within a block is nondeterministic (atomic scatter): compare per-block as multisets. csr_indices is over-allocated to B*T*H*S. Docstring mentions a radix-sort path for long problems that the code does not have. |
| causal_conv1d (ShortConvolution) forward: depthwise causal conv + bias + optional silu + residual, varlen, initial state | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | per sequence s (cu_seqlens segments, or batch rows): xs = x_s ([T_s, D]); prefix = h0[n, :, 1:].T if initial_state else zeros(W-1, D); xp = torch.cat([prefix, xs]).T[None]; y = F.conv1d(xp, weight[:, None, :], bias, groups=D)[0].T  (weight [D, W], y[t] = sum_i w[:, i] * x[t-W+1+i]); if activation in ('silu','swish'): y = F.silu(y); if residual: y = y + residual_s | yes | x [B,T,D] (any T/D strides), weight [D,W], initial_state [N,D,W] of which only slots 1..W-1 are read (slot W-1 = most recent token). weight=None means unweighted window sum. Residual added AFTER activation. Varlen: B==1, conv restarts per sequence. Gluon variant (opt-in FLA_GLUON / FLA_CONV_GLUON; W in {2,3,4}, no distributed, BlockedLayout only) has same semantics. |
| causal_conv1d backward (dx, dw, db, dh0, dresidual) | 反向 | torch 组合 |  | autograd of the causal_conv1d forward reference w.r.t. x, weight, bias, initial_state (and residual = identity), including gradient flowing from final_state (dht) back into x / initial_state | yes | silu backward recomputes the pre-activation. dht path: final_state slots that still hold initial_state (T < W) pass gradient straight to dh0 (compute_dh0_kernel). Gluon bwd (opt-in) rejects state gradients and reduces dw/db partials in causal_conv1d_bwd_kernel_dwdb. |
| causal_conv1d final-state extraction and single-token decode update | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | final_state[n] = torch.cat([h0[n] if initial_state else zeros(D, W), x_s.T], -1)[:, -W:]  (last W inputs, left-filled from initial_state then zeros); update: cache_new = torch.cat([cache[..., 1:], x[..., None]], -1); y = (cache_new * weight).sum(-1) + bias; y = F.silu(y) if silu; y = y + residual; cache updated in place | yes | Suspected deviation: causal_conv1d_update with cache=None never inserts x into the window (b_cache stays zero), so y = act(bias)+residual and x is ignored; with weight=None the wrapper fails (next_power_of_2(None)). Decode x may be [N,D], [1,N,D] or [N,1,D]. |
| chunk mean pooling over time (fixed chunk_size, varlen) fwd+bwd | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | per sequence, split time into consecutive chunks of chunk_size from the sequence start; o[c] = x[chunk c].mean(dim=time) (last partial chunk averaged over its actual length); output [B, NT, H, D] (varlen: [1, total_chunks, H, D]); bwd = autograd | yes | Layout [B,T,H,D]; fp32 accumulation. Used by NSA-style compression. |
| chunk-local and global cumsum (scalar [B,T,H] / vector [B,T,H,S], reverse, scale, varlen) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | global: o = scale * torch.cumsum(s, dim=1) per sequence (reverse: flip-cumsum-flip); local: split each sequence's time axis into consecutive chunks of chunk_size starting at the sequence start, o = scale * cumsum within each chunk (reverse: suffix sums within chunk); scale defaults to 1 | yes | Layout [B,T,H(,S)], fp32 accumulation, output_dtype default fp32. chunk_size must be a power of 2. Varlen: B==1 + cu_seqlens; chunks restart at each sequence start. Used by many attention ops with scale=1/ln2 (log2-space gates). |
| chunk_scaled_dot_kkt forward (beta * K K^T, strictly lower, optional scalar gate) [shared by DeltaNet, GDN chunk_size!=64, GDP, path_attn] | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | kh=k.repeat_interleave(HV//H,2); per sequence (batch row or cu_seqlens segment) split T into BT-chunks from its start; A[b,t,h,j]=beta[b,t,h]*(kh[b,t,h]@kh[b,s,h])*2**(g[b,t,h]-g[b,s,h]) with s=chunk_start(t)+j if s<t else 0 (g=None drops the 2** factor); output [B,T,HV,BT] in output_dtype (default fp32) | yes | g must be the log2-space chunk-local cumsum (callers apply RCP_LN2); base-2 is code-visible only (docstring says 'cumulative sum of the gate'). Rows/cols beyond T in the last chunk are 0. fp32 accumulation via tl.dot (TF32 allowed by default on sm>=80). |
| fused GRPO per-token loss fwd+bwd | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | grpo_loss_torch in fla/modules/grpo/ops.py: logp = log_softmax(logits[:, :-1], -1).gather(-1, input_ids[:, -L:]); kl = exp(ref_logp - logp) - (ref_logp - logp) - 1; loss = -(exp(logp - logp.detach()) * advantages[:, None] - beta * kl) * completion_mask; save_kl also returns kl * mask; gradient = autograd | yes | logits [B, L+1, V] (last position dropped, its grad zeroed), input_ids [B, K+L] (last L used), advantages [B], ref_logp [B,L]; masked tokens get loss 0 and zero grad. Forward value = beta*kl - advantage. inplace=True overwrites logits with grads. |
| fused KL divergence (student/teacher linear + KL, batchmean) fwd+bwd | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | F.kl_div(F.log_softmax(F.linear(x, weight), -1), F.log_softmax(F.linear(target_x, target_weight), -1), log_target=True, reduction='batchmean'); grads w.r.t. x, weight by autograd (teacher frozen); elementwise_mul_kernel scales precomputed grads by upstream scalar | yes | KL(teacher \|\| student) per row summed over V, divided by N for batchmean (only reduction supported). Logit gradient (p_s - p_t)/N is written in place into the logits buffer in the logits dtype (not fp32). target_x/target_weight must not require grad. |
| fused beta sigmoid gate (beta = scale * sigmoid(x), used by gated delta rule) fwd+bwd | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | y = scale * torch.sigmoid(x) (scale = 2.0 when allow_neg_eigval else 1.0 in gated_delta_rule); bwd = autograd | yes | Output fp32 regardless of input dtype; flat elementwise. |
| fused cross-entropy (per-row CE with label smoothing, logit scale, softcap, z-loss, ignore_index, vocab split/TP) fwd+bwd | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | z = logits * logit_scale; z = c * torch.tanh(z / c) if softcap c else z; lse = torch.logsumexp(z, -1); valid = target != ignore_index; loss = F.cross_entropy(z, target, ignore_index=ignore_index, label_smoothing=ls, reduction='none') + valid * lse_square_scale * lse**2; z_loss = valid * lse_square_scale * lse**2 (non-differentiable, returned for logging); module reduction 'mean' = loss.sum() / valid.sum(), 'sum' = loss.sum(); dlogits = autograd of loss | yes | logits [N,V], target [N] global indices; label smoothing divides by total classes (V*world_size under process_group vocab sharding). Loss/lse fp32. inplace_backward overwrites logits with dlogits. Gluon backend opt-in (FLA_GLUON/FLA_CE_GLUON), BlockedLayout only; FLA_USE_FAST_OPS=1 switches to fast_expf/fast_logf. |
| fused linear cross-entropy (chunked F.linear + CE; grads precomputed in forward, scaled by upstream grad) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | logits = F.linear(x, weight, bias); loss = fused cross-entropy reference on logits with (ignore_index, label_smoothing, logit_scale, logit_softcapping), reduced by 'mean' (sum / max(#valid, 1)) or 'sum'; grads = autograd; elementwise_mul_kernel computes grad * do (scalar) | yes | The CE math runs through the fused_cross_entropy kernels; this file's own launched kernels are the grad scaler and logsumexp (separate family). use_l2warp=True adds the L2Wrap custom gradient (max-logit * l2_penalty_factor / N at the argmax of the raw logits) with no forward-loss term, i.e. not the autograd of the loss - reference must reproduce fla/modules/l2warp.py. 'mean' clamps #valid to >=1 (torch would give NaN when all ignored). |
| fused norm + output gate (norm(x + residual) * act(g), act in swish/silu or sigmoid) fwd+bwd | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | h = x + residual (if given); n = F.layer_norm(h, (D,), weight, bias, eps) or F.rms_norm(h, (D,), weight, eps) + bias; y = n * F.silu(g) (activation 'swish'/'silu') or n * torch.sigmoid(g) ('sigmoid'); prenorm returns (y, h); bwd = autograd w.r.t. x, g, weight, bias, residual | yes | Gate applied AFTER norm (unlike layernorm_gated's optional pre-norm gating). No group support (weight [D]). Kernel silently applies no gate for an unknown activation string; modules reject it. |
| gated layer/rms norm with group_size (Mamba2 RMSNormGated: norm(x*silu(z)) or norm(x)*silu(z)) fwd+bwd | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | xg = x * F.silu(z) if (z is not None and not norm_before_gate) else x; grouped over last dim in chunks of group_size (default D): y = F.layer_norm(xg_group, (gs,), eps=eps) (or F.rms_norm) * weight_group + bias_group; if z is not None and norm_before_gate: y = y * F.silu(z); bwd = autograd | yes | Defaults differ per module: LayerNormGated norm_before_gate=True, RMSNormGated False. weight required (always loaded); bias optional. |
| l2norm over last dim fwd+bwd | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | y = x * torch.rsqrt((x * x).sum(-1, keepdim=True) + eps)  (eps default 1e-6); bwd = autograd | yes | NOT F.normalize: eps is added to the squared norm inside the sqrt (F.normalize uses max(\|\|x\|\|, eps)). Backward uses saved y and rstd: dx = rstd*(dy - (dy.y) y), exact autograd. D <= 64KB/elem_size. |
| layer/rms norm + BitNet int8 absmax activation fake-quant (fused BitLinear front-end) fwd+bwd | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | h = x + residual; n = F.layer_norm(h,(D,),w,b,eps) or F.rms_norm(h,(D,),w,eps)(+b); s = 127 / n.abs().amax(-1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1e-5); q = (n * s).round().clamp(-128, 127) / s; y = n + (q - n).detach()  (straight-through estimator, as in fla/modules/fused_bitlinear.py BitLinear); bwd = autograd of that (STE: quant has identity gradient) | yes | Deviation: kernel rounds half AWAY from zero (floor(x+0.5)/ceil(x-0.5), comment: triton-ascend lacks libdevice.round) while the repo's torch reference uses torch.round (half-to-even) -> differs at exact .5 ties. Weight 1.58-bit quant and F.linear are done in torch. |
| layer_norm / rms_norm / group_norm with fused residual add (prenorm) fwd+bwd | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | h = x + residual if residual is not None else x; LN: y = F.layer_norm(h, (D,), weight, bias, eps); RMS: y = F.rms_norm(h, (D,), weight, eps) + (bias if bias else 0); group (num_groups=G): hg = h.unflatten(-1, (G, D//G)); y = (F.layer_norm(hg, (D//G,), eps=eps) or F.rms_norm(hg, (D//G,), eps=eps)) * weight.view(G, -1) + bias.view(G, -1), flattened; prenorm returns (y, h); bwd = autograd (incl. dresidual passthrough) | yes | Biased variance, eps inside sqrt. weight/bias optional. residual add in fp32; residual_in_fp32 stores h in fp32. D <= 64KB/elem_size. *Linear variants (LayerNormLinear etc.) do F.linear in torch after the kernel. |
| logsumexp over last dim (optional scale, optional tanh softcap) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | z = x * scale if scale else x; z = c * torch.tanh(z / c) if softcapping c else z; torch.logsumexp(z, dim=-1) | yes | Same kernel name in two files (utils version has no softcap). Kernel produces per-block partial lse [N, ceil(D/BD)], reduced with torch .logsumexp(-1). Output fp32 unless dtype given. |
| matmul / addmm utility (optional fused activation) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | matmul(a, b, activation): act(a @ b) with act in {'' (none), 'relu': F.relu, 'leaky_relu': F.leaky_relu(.,0.01), 'sigmoid': torch.sigmoid, 'tanh': torch.tanh}; addmm(x, a, b, alpha, beta): alpha*(a @ b) + beta*x (x broadcast as [N] bias or [M,N]); a 2D or batched 3D, b 2D | yes | Not used inside fla. tanh is implemented as (e^x-e^-x)/(e^x+e^-x) -> NaN for \|x\| > ~88 (fp32 exp overflow). alpha/beta are read with tl.load, so they must be passed as 1-element tensors despite the float type hint. tf32 allowed for non-fp32 inputs. |
| pack / unpack padded sequences (left or right padding) via cu_seqlens | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | pack: torch.cat([x[b, S - L_b:] if padding_side == 'left' else x[b, :L_b] for b in range(B)]) with L_b = diff(cu_seqlens); unpack: inverse scatter into zeros([N, S, ...]); bwd of each = the other | yes | Pure data movement; default padding_side='left'. |
| rotary embedding (non-interleaved / interleaved, partial rotary dim, seqlen offsets, varlen) fwd+bwd | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | rotary_embedding_ref(x, cos[pos], sin[pos], interleaved) from fla/modules/rotary/ops.py: x[..., :2R]*cos_rep + rotate_half(x[..., :2R], interleaved)*sin_rep, x[..., 2R:] unchanged, with pos = t (position within each sequence; restarts at 0 per cu_seqlens segment) + seqlen_offsets (int or per-sequence [N]); bwd = same op with sin negated (conjugate=True) | yes | x [B,T,H,D], cos/sin [TR, R] (rotary dim 2R <= D <= 256), dtypes must match x. Positions with pos<0 or >=TR (left-padding via negative offsets) are left ZERO in the rotated part when not inplace (unchanged when inplace) - a code-visible convention. |
| softmax attention one-step decoding (packed varlen KV, GQA, window, FoX gate, sink) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | For each sequence n with keys k[0,bos:eos] (restricted to the last window_size keys if set): s_j = scale*<q[0,n,hq],k[j,hq//G]> (+ Gc[eos-1]-Gc[j], Gc=cumsum(g[0,bos:eos,hq]*(scale if do_gate_scale else 1))); o[0,n,hq]=softmax_j(s) @ v (sink_bias as extra denominator-only logit); zeros if no visible key (T_n==0 or window_size==0). | yes | Query sits at the last position of each KV segment (bottom-right alignment, no causal mask needed). do_gate_scale=True follows the PaTH-FoX convention (gate multiplied by scale). Minor deviation: q*scale is rounded to the input dtype before the dot (parallel kernel scales the fp32 logits). Forward-only. |
| softmax attention parallel backward (delta preprocess, dq, dk/dv, dg) | 反向 | torch 组合 |  | torch.autograd.grad of the forward reference w.r.t. (q,k,v,g,sink_bias) | yes | Recomputes P from saved log2-lse; delta=rowsum(o*do) (preprocess kernel is also reused by PaTH backward). dk/dv computed per q-head (fp32 when GQA) then summed over the group; dg obtained as reverse cumsum of per-position dg_cumsum; dsink computed in torch. Backward BK/BV = next_pow2(K/V) uncapped, so very large head dims may exceed sm86 shared memory. |
| softmax attention parallel forward (causal, GQA, sliding window, FoX gate, sink bias, varlen) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | Per sequence (loop over cu_seqlens segments when varlen, B==1): Q=q.transpose(1,2); K=k.transpose(1,2).repeat_interleave(HQ//H,1); V=v.transpose(1,2).repeat_interleave(HQ//H,1); S=scale*Q@K.mT; if g: Gc=g.cumsum(1).transpose(1,2); S=S+Gc[...,:,None]-Gc[...,None,:]; mask=(j<=i)&(i-j<W if window_size else True); S=S.masked_fill(~mask,-inf); if sink_bias: P=torch.softmax(torch.cat([S,sink_bias.view(1,HQ,1,1).expand(*S.shape[:-1],1)],-1),-1)[...,:-1] else P=torch.softmax(S,-1); o=(P@V).transpose(1,2). Plain case (g,window,sink=None): F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q.transpose(1,2),k.transpose(1,2),v.transpose(1,2),is_causal=True,scale=scale,enable_gqa=True).transpose(1,2) | yes | Pinned by docstring: [B,T,HQ,K]/[B,T,H,K] layout, scale=K^-0.5, GQA head h->h//(HQ/H), window keeps keys [i-W+1,i], g is per-q-head log decay cumsum'd per sequence (not scaled by `scale`), sink adds exp(sink) to denominator only. Square (Tq==Tk) top-left causal. Internally logits in log2 units (RCP_LN2); lse saved in log2 base. Asserts K<=256 (BK cap). sm86 takes smallest-tile branch (99KB smem<ampere threshold). parallel_forgetting_attn is a thin wrapper over this kernel. |
| solve_tril: per-chunk inverse (I + A)^-1 of strictly-lower-triangular A (16/32/64 blocks) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | for each chunk of BT rows (BT = A.shape[-1] in {16,32,64}; chunks restart per sequence) and head: M = torch.tril(A_chunk[:, :L], -1) (L = valid rows in chunk); Ai_chunk = torch.linalg.solve_triangular(torch.eye(L) + M, torch.eye(L), upper=False) (== torch.linalg.inv(I + M)); entries above the diagonal and rows/cols beyond T are 0 | yes | A [B,T,H,BT]; diagonal and upper part of A are ignored (masked). Output dtype fp32 by default. Optional TMA path (FLA_USE_TMA=1 and cc>=9) and tf32 dot precision (FLA_TRIL_PRECISION) are off on sm86; default ieee dots. |
| stick-breaking attention parallel backward | 反向 | torch 组合 |  | torch.autograd.grad of the stick-breaking reference (o and rem outputs) w.r.t. (q,k,v) | yes | dq kernel stores per-key-block snapshots of log remaining stick and delta ([B,T,HQ,NS]) consumed by the dkv kernel; same approximate softplus2. |
| stick-breaking attention parallel forward | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | z=scale*Q@K^T (GQA repeat_interleave); M=(j<i) or (j<=i if attend_current); lom=F.logsigmoid(-z).masked_fill(~M,0); logA=F.logsigmoid(z)+lom.flip(-1).cumsum(-1).flip(-1)-lom; A=logA.masked_fill(~M,-inf).exp(); o=A@V; rem=lom.sum(-1).exp(); per cu_seqlens segment when varlen | yes | Docstring/README pin nearest-key-first breaking, unnormalised weights, attend_current flag, scale=K^-0.5, returns (o, rem). K,V<=256; Tq must equal Tk (no decoding). Deviation: log2-space with approximate PTX softplus2 (ex2/lg2.approx, identity above 15) on NVIDIA. |

Bwd kernels also handle grad_cache_in from a next context-parallel rank. |

Pipeline: chunk_local_cumsum(g)*RCP_LN2 -> common chunk_fwd_h -> these kernels (exp2 internally). chunk_gla_fwd_kernel_o is also used by chunk_rwkv6 and chunk_gsa (via chunk_gla_fwd). naive_recurrent_gla: fp32, fixed scale, no varlen/state_v_first. fused_chunk_gla is deprecated (raises NotImplementedError); fused_recurrent_gla uses the common fused_recurrent kernels (gk and gv). |

Docstring gives g_atk/beta_atk [B,T,H], A_log/log_atk_scale [H] but kernel indexes them by value head i_hv (shape HV) - mismatch when HV>H. use_qk_l2norm_in_kernel defaults True. In-kernel gate requires A_log (no A_log=None form). |
| RWKV4 fused_recurrent WKV (fwd+bwd) | 前向+反向 | 需要定义 | A | decay parameterisation w_eff = -exp(w) applied in wrapper；bonus u for current token；state = (alpha, beta, eps) running-max-stabilised numerator/denominator, shape [B,3,1,C]; meaning of a fresh state (eps=-inf?) unspecified；returns only last state though all per-step states are computed | yes | No naive reference, no docstring, no tests, not exported from fla.ops and unused by layers -> low priority/likely legacy. Layout [B,T,C] (no heads). gw = grad_weff * w_eff (chain rule through -exp) inside kernel. |
| RWKV6 chunk backward | 反向 | 需要定义 | A | w domain: log-decay (w = -exp(w_raw) applied in layer; docstring 'in log space')；bonus u [H,K] applied to current token: o_t = q_t^T (S_{t-1} + Diag(u) k_t v_t^T)；du reduction over B,T; dh0 | yes | Recomputes h in backward; own dh kernel (not common chunk_bwd_dh). |
| RWKV6 chunk forward (cumsum, intra-chunk A) | 前向 | 需要定义 | A | w domain: log-decay (w = -exp(w_raw) applied in layer; docstring 'in log space')；bonus u [H,K] applied to current token: o_t = q_t^T (S_{t-1} + Diag(u) k_t v_t^T)；scale default K**-0.5 vs RWKV convention 1.0 (layers pass scale=1)；inclusive/exclusive cumsum pair (gi, ge) pre-scaled by 1/ln2 | yes | Pinned by docstring: [B,T,H,*] layout (head_first removed), scale default K**-0.5, h0/ht [N,H,K,V] fp32, cu_seqlens FlashAttention-style with B==1 and one state per sequence. Uses common chunk_fwd_h and GLA chunk_gla_fwd_kernel_o for the output. Naive refs are head-first [B,H,T,K], fp32; naive_chunk_rwkv6 has no scale (=1) while naive_recurrent_rwkv6 defaults K**-0.5. A tilelang backend for fwd_intra exists (not Triton). |
| RWKV6 fused_recurrent (fwd+bwd) | 前向+反向 | 需要定义 | A | w domain: log-decay (w = -exp(w_raw) applied in layer; docstring 'in log space')；bonus u [H,K] applied to current token: o_t = q_t^T (S_{t-1} + Diag(u) k_t v_t^T)；scale default K**-0.5 vs RWKV convention 1.0 (layers pass scale=1)；reverse=True scan semantics | yes | Pinned by docstring: [B,T,H,*] layout (head_first removed), scale default K**-0.5, h0/ht [N,H,K,V] fp32, cu_seqlens FlashAttention-style with B==1 and one state per sequence. dw via separate reverse-cumsum kernel. |
| RWKV7 fused_mul_recurrent forward (kk/a parameterised DPLR) | 前向 | 需要定义 | A | in-kernel transition a_act = -kk, b = kk*a, i.e. S_t = Diag(exp(w_t))S_{t-1} - (kk*a)(kk^T S_{t-1}) + k v^T；w is already log-decay (layer uses w = -exp(-0.5)*sigmoid(.), paper d=exp(-exp(w)))；scale: signature default 1.0 but None -> K^-0.5；reverse option; T==1 decode fast path；varlen cu_seqlens (B==1) | yes | Forward-only (no autograd). chunk_rwkv7 / fused_recurrent_rwkv7 are thin wrappers over the DPLR chunk / fused_recurrent kernels (counted in DPLR families). |
| ReBased parallel (fwd+bwd) | 前向+反向 | 需要定义 | A | kernel A_ij = (scale*q.k)^2 (learnable norm/affine of paper applied outside)；eps: API default 1e-5 vs naive 1e-6；use_scale flag (scale K**-0.5 or 1)；return_both returns (o,z) still in [B,H,T,*] layout | yes | K<=128. No varlen/h0. No dedicated test (not covered in tests/ops). Suspected deviations: eps mismatch with naive; return_both layout not transposed back to [B,T,H,*]. |
| Simple GLA parallel (quadratic form, fwd+bwd) | 前向+反向 | 需要定义 | A | gate domain: head-wise natural-log forget gate g [B,T,H], inclusive cumsum G, decay exp(G_i-G_j) for j<=i (README recurrence S_t=g_t S_{t-1}+k_t v_t^T)；g=None -> plain causal (unnormalised) linear attention；output_attentions returns scale*qk^T*decay [B,H,T,T], dense only；varlen = block-diagonal per sequence; no h0/ht | yes | Closed form exists once conventions are accepted: o = (tril(scale*q k^T * exp(G_i-G_j))) v -- naive_parallel_simple_gla (fp32, head-first). chunk_size fixed 128. Caller parallel_retention passes g=log(1-2^(-5-h)) broadcast. Tile sizes from check_shared_mem (A6000 gets 64-wide tiles). |
| TTT-Linear chunk backward | 反向 | 需要定义 | A | inner model: linear W with bias, LayerNorm(kW+b) using the SAME (w,b) as the output norm_residual；reconstruction target v - k and loss gradient form；per-token learning rate eta [B,T,H,1] applied as mini-batch GD with eta of the last token for the state update；gradients w.r.t. w, b, eta and both initial states | yes | Recomputes states; includes a torch reference for the LN backward step. |
| TTT-Linear chunk forward | 前向 | 需要定义 | A | inner model: linear W with bias, LayerNorm(kW+b) using the SAME (w,b) as the output norm_residual；reconstruction target v - k and loss gradient form；per-token learning rate eta [B,T,H,1] applied as mini-batch GD with eta of the last token for the state update；mini_batch_size = chunk_size (default 16); partial last mini-batch semantics (naive pads and copies eta)；LN eps 1e-6, K must equal V, K<=128；initial_state_bias / final_state_bias semantics | yes | Pinned by docstring: [B,T,H,*] layout (head_first removed), scale default K**-0.5, h0/ht [N,H,K,V] fp32, cu_seqlens FlashAttention-style with B==1 and one state per sequence. Output post-processed by norm_residual (fla group_norm module kernel, other family). naive is fp32 and uses explicit padding. NK==NV==1 asserted. |
| TTT-Linear fused_chunk (fwd+bwd) | 前向+反向 | 需要定义 | A | inner model: linear W with bias, LayerNorm(kW+b) using the SAME (w,b) as the output norm_residual；reconstruction target v - k and loss gradient form；per-token learning rate eta [B,T,H,1] applied as mini-batch GD with eta of the last token for the state update；mini_batch_size = chunk_size (default 16); partial last mini-batch semantics (naive pads and copies eta)；LN eps 1e-6, K must equal V, K<=128；initial_state_bias / final_state_bias semantics | yes | Same semantics as chunk_ttt_linear in a single fused kernel; varlen supported. |
| Wall attention cached decode (pre-rescaled KV cache) | 前向 | 需要定义 | B | causal alignment among T_q>1 queries: kernel applies NO causal mask (every query sees all T_kv keys) and no window；input contract: k_tilde/r_cache must equal build_wall_kv_cache(k, P, C) and p_curr the matching prefix rows；returned lse in log2 base | yes | For T_q==1 equals the Wall reference over the whole cache. Suspected deviation: layer calls it with q_len>1 after prefill, so earlier new tokens attend to later new keys. With g_scalar the row mask is overwritten by o_q_global<T_kv. Asserts K<=256. |
| [Ascend] GDN WY representation (recompute_w_u fwd, split prepare_wy_repr bwd) | 前向+反向 | 需要定义 | A | same as CUDA GDN chunk WY family (per-chunk w,u; natural-log dg) | no | NPU-only dispatch; backward split into 6 kernels launched via core-grid helper; uses Ascend-only slice builtins. |
| [Ascend] GDN2 chunk backward (WY) | 反向 | 需要定义 | A | same as GDN2 chunk backward | no | Triton-Ascend (Huawei NPU) backend: dispatched only when IS_NPU; tuned for NPU UB/core grid. Replaces chunk_gdn2_bwd_wy_dqkg_fused. |
| [Ascend] GDN2 chunk forward (intra/solve) | 前向 | 需要定义 | A | same as GDN2 chunk forward | no | Triton-Ascend (Huawei NPU) backend: dispatched only when IS_NPU; tuned for NPU UB/core grid. Replaces chunk_gdn2_fwd_intra. |
| [Ascend] GDN2 fused_recurrent forward | 前向 | 需要定义 | A | same as GDN2 fused_recurrent forward | no | Triton-Ascend (Huawei NPU) backend: dispatched only when IS_NPU; tuned for NPU UB/core grid. |
| [Ascend] GLA chunk (fwd+bwd) | 前向+反向 | 需要定义 | A | same as GLA chunk forward/backward | no | Triton-Ascend NPU backend for the GLA chunk sub-ops (registered via @register('gla'), NPU only); uses tl.extract_slice. |
| [Ascend] KDA chunk backward | 反向 | 需要定义 | A | same as KDA chunk backward | no | Triton-Ascend (Huawei NPU) backend: dispatched only when IS_NPU; tuned for NPU UB/core grid. Splits wy_dqkg_fused into v/k/dw/dA-finalize stages. |
| [Ascend] KDA chunk forward (intra/solve, token-parallel, WY) | 前向 | 需要定义 | A | same as KDA chunk forward | no | Triton-Ascend (Huawei NPU) backend: dispatched only when IS_NPU; tuned for NPU UB/core grid. Replaces chunk_kda_fwd_intra / token_parallel / recompute_w_u_fwd. |
