## flash-attention (Triton code)

https://github.com/Dao-AILab/flash-attention，提交 `1fc0221183e7`，许可 BSD-3-Clause；`@triton.jit` 函数 27 个，入口 kernel 13 个。

| 分族 | 方向 | 类别 | 规格类别 | 参照程序，或待定事项 | sm_86 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| flash_attn_triton attention (bias + causal) | 前向+反向 | torch 运算 |  | o = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q.transpose(1,2), k.transpose(1,2), v.transpose(1,2), attn_mask=bias_bcast(+ top-left causal -inf mask torch.ones(Sq,Sk).tril().logical_not() if causal and bias given), is_causal=(causal and bias is None), scale=softmax_scale or 1/sqrt(d)).transpose(1,2); lse = torch.logsumexp(scale*q@k^T + bias + causal_mask, -1); backward = autograd grads of o w.r.t. q,k,v (no bias grad) | yes | Layout (B,S,H,D); fp16/bf16 only; d<=128; no GQA, no dropout, no varlen. Bias shape (B\|1,H\|1,1\|Sq,Sk) in q dtype or fp32 ('vector' vs 'matrix'), added after scaling. Causal mask is TOP-LEFT aligned (offs_m >= offs_n), matching SDPA is_causal (unlike FA2 CUDA bottom-right). LSE stored in (B,H,ceil(Sq/128)*128) fp32 buffer. bwd: autotune may pick SEQUENCE_PARALLEL=True -> dq via tl.atomic_add (non-deterministic order); docstring warns of possible compiler race conditions for untested headdims. Uses removed Triton API tl.dot(..., trans_b=True) -> needs triton 2.0-era compiler or patch; hardware-wise plain (sm80-tested, fine on sm86). |
| flash_attn_triton_og (Triton tutorial fused attention, causal only) | 前向+反向 | torch 运算 |  | F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True, scale=sm_scale); backward = autograd grads w.r.t. q,k,v | yes | Benchmark-only copy. Layout (B,H,N,D); always causal; no masking so N_CTX must be a multiple of 128; D in {16,32,64,128}; K/V offsets computed with Q strides (q,k,v must share layout/strides); fwd stores l and m separately; bwd pre-divides dO by l. Uses removed tl.dot trans_a/trans_b API. |
| cross_entropy (label smoothing, logit scale, z-loss, TP split) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | z = logit_scale*logits.double(); lse = torch.logsumexp(z,-1); loss = F.cross_entropy(z, labels, reduction='none', label_smoothing=smoothing, ignore_index=ignore_index) + torch.where(labels!=ignore_index, lse_square_scale*lse**2, 0); z_loss = torch.where(labels!=ignore_index, lse_square_scale*lse**2, 0); backward = autograd of loss w.r.t. logits (z_loss gets no grad) | yes | logits (rows, vocab) any float dtype, computed in fp32; per-row losses (reduction='none'). Smoothing convention = torch's (s*sum/C over total_classes). Deviation: non-ignored labels outside [0,n_cols) drop the CE term (loss = smoothing*(lse - mean logit) + z-loss) instead of raising like torch. PRECOMPUTED_LSE path only when smoothing==0 and logit_scale==1. SPLIT (process_group) path: kernel computes per-shard partial loss without LSE; final loss assembled in torch after all-reduce -> reference is the full-vocab loss, needs multi-rank emulation. inplace_backward overwrites logits. |
| fused (residual+dropout+rowscale) LayerNorm / RMSNorm, dropout_p == 0 | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | layer_norm_ref / rms_norm_ref in the same file with dropout_p=0: h = x*rowscale[:,None] (+ x1) (+ residual); w' = w+1 if zero_centered_weight; y = F.layer_norm(h, (N,), w', b, eps) \| F.rms_norm(h, (N,), w', eps) (+ b); y1 likewise with weight1/bias1; residual_out = h; backward = autograd (dw/db partials summed over programs) | yes | Rows of a 2D view, N*elem_size < 64KB; stats in fp32; mean/rstd saved. RMSNorm optionally has a bias added after scaling. residual_out may be stored in a different (e.g. fp32) dtype; x1 and rowscale mutually exclusive. bwd can RECOMPUTE_OUTPUT y; dw/db written as per-program partials then summed in torch. |
| fused linear + activation (fwd) and activation-grad dgrad (bwd) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | fwd: pre = F.linear(x, weight, bias); out = act(pre) with act in {id, F.gelu(pre), F.gelu(pre, approximate='tanh'), F.relu(pre)**2}; act_input = pre if save_act_input. bwd (triton_dgrad_act): grad_input = (grad_output @ weight) * act'(act_input), i.e. autograd of the fwd w.r.t. x | yes | Same dtype for x/weight/bias; fp32 accumulate. Bug/deviation: kernel_fwd stores C without mask (mask computed but unused) -> out-of-bounds writes when M or N not divisible by BLOCK_M/BLOCK_N (reads are wrapped via rm%M). gelu uses tl.libdevice.erf and module imports triton.ops.matmul_perf_model -> both removed in modern Triton (3.x); needs old Triton. squared_relu_grad uses 2x for x>=0. Only 'id','gelu','gelu_approx','squared_relu' accepted by wrappers. |
| rotary embedding (RoPE apply) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | flash_attn.layers.rotary.apply_rotary_emb_torch: ro=2*cos.shape[-1]; c=repeat(cos[pos],'s d->s 1 (2 d)') (interleaved: '(d 2)'); out = cat([x[...,:ro]*c + rotate_half(x[...,:ro], interleaved)*s, x[...,ro:]], -1) with pos = arange(seqlen)+seqlen_offsets[b]; conjugate=True uses -sin (this is the backward) | yes | x (B,S,H,D) or varlen (total,H,D)+cu_seqlens (positions restart at 0 per sequence, plus seqlen_offsets int or (B,) tensor). cos/sin (seqlen_ro, rotary_dim/2) supplied by caller; rotary_dim<=headdim<=256, dims beyond rotary_dim copied through. Interleaved=GPT-J pairs, else NeoX halves. Computes in fp32. Positions >= seqlen_ro masked to cos=1,sin=0 (identity) rather than error (only asserted for int offsets). Backward is the same kernel with conjugate=True. Needs triton>=3.0 (tl.split/tl.join). |
| fused LayerNorm / RMSNorm with dropout (dropout_p > 0) | 前向+反向 | 随机 | R | RNG stream is tl.rand(seed_row, col, n_rounds=7) Philox with per-row int64 seeds from torch.randint; not bit-reproducible by torch | yes | Deterministic checks: with return_dropout_mask=True compare to the ref using the stored masks (inverted-dropout scaling 1/(1-p)); keep-rate ~ 1-p statistically; backward regenerates the same masks from the saved seeds -> check dx against autograd with the stored masks; p=0 reduces to the deterministic family. |

## gemlite

https://github.com/mobiusml/gemlite，提交 `f649e80e3014`，许可 Apache-2.0；`@triton.jit` 函数 48 个，入口 kernel 34 个。

| 分族 | 方向 | 类别 | 规格类别 | 参照程序，或待定事项 | sm_86 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| bitpack_pack (over cols / over rows) | — | torch 组合 |  | cols: (W.view(R, C//e, e).long() << (torch.arange(e)*nbits)).sum(-1).to(D); rows: (W.view(R//e, e, C).long() << (torch.arange(e)*nbits).view(1,e,1)).sum(1).to(D); e=packing_bits//nbits, D={8:uint8,16:int16,32:int32,64:int64}[packing_bits] (two's-complement wrap), optional .t() | yes | Convention (LSB-first, non-overlapping fields so sum==OR) fixed by the kernel and the library's torch twin. Inputs must be in [0, 2^nbits); no masking: requires C (resp. R) divisible by e; unroll=largest pow2 divisor (<=64). Only the over-cols variant is used (GemLiteLinearTriton.pack). |
| bitpack_unpack (over cols / over rows) | — | torch 组合 |  | cols: ((P.unsqueeze(-1) >> (torch.arange(e)*nbits)) & (2**nbits-1)).view(R, C*e).to(dtype); rows: ((P.unsqueeze(-1) >> (torch.arange(e)*nbits)) & (2**nbits-1)).permute(0,2,1).reshape(R*e, C).to(dtype) | yes | Arithmetic shift on signed containers is harmless thanks to the mask. Exposed as torch.library custom ops gemlite::unpack_over_{cols,rows}_triton; not used inside the matmul path. unpack_over_cols_torch_fake references an undefined name (num_output_cols) - fake-tensor bug only. |
| act_quant_mxfp4 (e2m1 packed + E8M0, group 32) | 前向 | 需要定义 | F | shared exponent = ceil(log2(amax/6)) clamped >= 2^-30 (not the OCP floor rule)；e2m1 rounding: threshold count \|x\|>{0.25,0.75,1.25,1.75,2.5,3.5,5.0} (ties go down) vs PTX cvt.rn.satfinite (RNE) path vs torch twin argmin (ties to first table entry)；sign of zero: kernel encodes small negatives as -0 (code 8), torch twin as +0 (code 0)；nibble order: even k in low nibble; output [M, K/2] uint8; scales [M_pad32, K/32] uint8；padding-row scales: 127 (v5) vs uninitialised (v1-v3) | yes | Default threshold path uses only fp32 compares/bit ops -> runs on sm86; the optional ptx_pack path (GEMLITE_ENABLE_PTX_FP4_PACK, cvt e2m1x2, CUDA13 ptxas) and TMA option (v1 use_tma, off) are Blackwell-only. 8th threshold 7.0 is a dummy; no saturation guard needed since scale>=amax/6. Dispatch uses v5. |
| act_quant_mxfp8 (e4m3 + E8M0, group 32) | 前向 | 需要定义 | F | shared exponent = ceil(log2(amax/448)) (bitwise next-pow2), clamped to [2^-30, 2^127] - differs from OCP MX rule floor(log2 amax) - emax_elem；element cast: clamp to +-448 then RNE to e4m3fn；scale tensor rows padded to multiple of 32; padding-row scale value: 127 (v4), computed from zeros (v2), uninitialised (v1/v3), 2^-30 in torch twin；v3/v4 multiply by reciprocal of the power-of-two scale (exact) | no | Writes float8_e4m3fn output (Triton fp8e4nv needs sm89+). Dispatch uses v4. v1 is flat-1D over contiguous groups of 32. |
| act_quant_nvfp4 (e2m1 packed + e4m3 scale, group 16, global meta scale) | 前向 | 需要定义 | F | global meta_scale = 448*6/amax_tensor (dynamic, v6 fused) or caller-provided static value (GEMLITE_FAST_NVFP4 default 448)；block scale = e4m3(min(amax_blk*meta/6, 448)) (RNE); effective scale = max(e4m3/meta, 1e-6)；e2m1 rounding: threshold (ties down) vs PTX RNE path vs torch-twin argmin；no saturation in threshold path: with static meta and clamped scale \|x/scale\|>7 yields code 8/16 (overflows into sign/neighbour nibble) while torch twin saturates to +-6；zero blocks: kernel clamps scale to eps; torch twin divides by 0 (NaN)；padding-row scales = 1.0 (v5/v6) vs uninitialised (v1-v3) | no | Writes float8_e4m3fn scales (sm89+). Dispatch: v6 fused (dynamic meta, grid<=num_SMs, atomic_max + spin-wait grid barrier using only relaxed atomics - memory-ordering of the meta_scale store is not guaranteed by fences) or v5 when meta_scale given. Returns (q, scales, meta_scale); GEMM uses 1/(meta_w*meta_x). |
| act_quant_per_block (int8/fp8, 1x128 groups) | 前向 | 需要定义 | F | group = contiguous 128 elements along K per row (BLOCK_QUANT_SIZE), K % 128 == 0 asserted；scale = max(amax/qmax, 1e-6) fp32, layout [M, K/128]；int8 tie rounding (libdevice.round vs torch.round half-even)；v2 multiplies by reciprocal | yes | Feeds channel_scale_mode 5 matmuls. Dispatch uses v2. sm86 for int8; fp8 target needs sm89+. |
| act_quant_per_token (int8/fp8 absmax per row) | 前向 | 需要定义 | F | scale = max(amax_row/qmax, 1e-6) in fp32 (fp32_scale=True); q = clamp(x/scale, qmin, qmax)；int8 tie rounding: libdevice.round (half away from zero) on NVIDIA, floor(x+0.5) on AMD vs torch.round (half-even) in the torch twin；fp8 target: plain cast (RNE) after clamp to finfo range; e4m3fn vs e5m2 / fnuz on AMD；v4 multiplies by 1/scale instead of dividing (last-ulp differences) | yes | Returns (q[M,K], scales[M,1]). Dispatch uses v3 (persistent). v1-v3 load the whole row (BLOCK_SIZE_K=next_pow2(K)); v4 two-pass streaming. sm86 for int8 target; fp8 targets need sm89+. |
| bitplane_lowbit_gemm (experimental A16W3 / A16W5) | 前向 | 需要定义 | G | bit-plane split: q = (hi << 1) \| lo with lo = 1-bit plane, hi = 2-bit (W3) or 4-bit (W5) plane, each int32-packed over K LSB-first；asymmetric dequant (q - z)*s, scales/zeros (K/group_size, N)；fp16 accumulation by default (acc_dtype=tl.float16), output dtype = scales dtype | yes | Not imported by the package (benchmark scripts; module-level hqq/bitblas imports). Loads have no K or N masks: requires K % BLOCK_SIZE_K == 0 and N % BLOCK_SIZE_N == 0. tl.dot input_precision='ieee'. |
| int_dequant_matmul (A16Wn / A8Wn / A8W8, grouped or channel/block scaled) | 前向 | 需要定义 | G | packing: K packed into int32 (default; 8/16/64 possible), element k at row k//e, bit shift (k%e)*W_nbits (LSB-first), e=packing_bits/W_nbits, W_nbits in {1,2,4,8}; 16-bit = unpacked fp16；W_group_mode: 0 none, 1 Wq-z, 2 Wq*s, 3 (Wq-z)*s, 4 fma Wq*s+z' with z'=-z*s precomputed (rounded to meta dtype) by GemLiteLinearTriton.pack(fma_mode=True)；define reference at kernel level (z' given) or module level ((Wq-z)*s): differs by rounding of z*s；scale/zero layout (K/group_size, N), group g=k//group_size; scalar zero allowed (zero_is_scalar, integer subtraction before cast)；channel_scale_mode: 1 out*s_w[n], 2 out*s_x[m], 3 both, 5 = 128x128 weight block scales (fp32) x per-(row,128-col) activation scales (fp32), BLOCK_QUANT_SIZE=128；dequant arithmetic precision: done in meta dtype (fp16/bf16) then cast to input dtype before dot; fp64 reference dequantises exactly；accumulator dtype (fp32 default, fp16 via GEMLITE_FAST_GEMV_ACC / acc_dtype; int32 for int8xint8)；GEMV dump_b_val=0.001 pre-scale for fp16-acc 8-bit (numerical trick) | yes | Candidate ref: Wq=unpack(B); W=dequant(Wq,s,z,mode) per group; C=A@W; then channel scaling; mode5: C=sum_kb (A_kb@W_kb)*sa[m,kb]*sb[kb,n//128]. Inputs: A fp16/bf16 (int8/fp8 when scaled_activations), K%32==0, K%group_size==0, group_size>=16. sm86: fp16/bf16/int8 paths plain tl.dot; fp8 input dtypes need sm89+. Split-K/GEMV variants use tl.atomic_add into zero-inited output (fp32 buffer when bf16 atomics unsupported, i.e. sm<90): summation order nondeterministic. Suspected issue: GEMM/GEMM_SPLITK pruners force BLOCK_SIZE_K=max(min(bk,g),32), so group_size=16 (allowed by GemLiteLinearTriton) gives BLOCK_SIZE_K>group_size and k_m=k*BK/g skips every other group. gemm_splitK_persistent_kernel is not dispatched (core uses gemm_splitK) and its launcher omits a_sizeof/b_sizeof positionally (broken); it also skips dequant when elements_per_sample==1 and has no mode 5. Bias added outside kernel. |
| mx_dequant_gemv (software-dequant MX GEMV) | 前向 | 需要定义 | G | e2m1 decode via 16-entry int8 table holding 2x values, compensated by E8M0 decode exp2(s-127)*0.5；NVFP4 global meta_scale is NOT applied in this kernel (deviation vs mx_scaled_matmul)；activation scales: per-group (mode 4) or per-token (mode 2) | yes | Plain loads/gather/exp2 + atomic_add, M==1 oriented. sm86 only for fp16/bf16-activation MXFP4 (uint8 E8M0 scales); MXFP8 weights/activations and NVFP4 (e4m3 scales) need fp8 support (sm89+). Code comment: 'gemv not generating correct results with mxfp dtypes except A16W4'; tests exclude GEMV for MX. |
| mx_scaled_matmul (MXFP8/MXFP4/NVFP4 via tl.dot_scaled) | 前向 | 需要定义 | G | element formats: weights e4m3 (8-bit) or e2m1 packed 2/byte (low nibble = even k); activations fp16/bf16 (unscaled), e4m3 or e2m1；scale formats: E8M0 uint8 (bias 127) for MX group 32; e4m3 fp8 for NVFP4 group 16；NVFP4 global meta scale: acc *= meta_scale_norm = 1/(meta_w*meta_x) (or 1/meta_w weight-only)；scale layout: (N, K/g) 2D or preshuffled 5D TMA layout [1,N/128,K_S/4,2,256] (core.py pack)；MXFP8 post-scale variant: channel_scale_mode 2 multiplies per-token scale after dot；dot_scaled internal rounding/accumulation semantics (fp32 acc) | no | Candidate ref: (fp4/fp8 decode(Wq) * 2^(e8m0-127) or e4m3 scale) per group, same for activations, C = Xdeq @ Wdeq.T [* 1/(meta_w*meta_x) for NVFP4]. Uses tl.dot_scaled, fp8 dtypes, optional TMA tensor descriptors (5D scales) -> Blackwell-class path. Suspected tail-K bug in gemm_MX_kernel non-TMA path: offs_ak/offs_bk advanced by BLOCK_SIZE_K and masked against K although they index packed (K/e) elements (gemm_splitK_MX_kernel does it correctly); matters only when K % BLOCK_SIZE_K != 0. |

## kernl

https://github.com/ELS-RD/kernl，提交 `0347bec7ebea`，许可 Apache-2.0；`@triton.jit` 函数 30 个，入口 kernel 22 个。

| 分族 | 方向 | 类别 | 规格类别 | 参照程序，或待定事项 | sm_86 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| batched matmul | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.bmm(a, b)  (a [B,M,K], b [B,K,N], contiguous) | yes | BUG: K mask uses k_range_offs < K (not k + k_range_offs), so K tail is never masked; K must be a multiple of BLOCK_K (32) (assert commented out). Accumulator always cast to fp16 before store, so fp32 inputs get fp16-rounded results. Not wired into the optimizer. |
| experimental llama: rmsnorm | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.rms_norm(x, (K,), rms_w, eps=1e-6)  (== pytorch_reference.rms_norm_pytorch) | yes | Experimental; module-level test code runs on import. eps and N are constexpr. |
| experimental misc: matrix-vector product perf kernel | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.mv(matrix.float(), vec.float())  (both IS_DOT paths) | yes | Perf experiment; IS_DOT path uses tl.dot(trans_b=True) (Triton 2.0 API). Every program writes the same output. |
| experimental misc: transpose test kernel | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | x.t()  (single block, SIZE_M x D_HEAD constexpr, no masks) | yes | Script-level test, grid (1,). |
| experimental stream-K matmul (first wave + full tiles) | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.mm(a, b) | yes | Old experiment. No masks: M, N, K must be multiples of BLK_M/BLK_N/BLK_K. Partial tiles combined via fp16 tl.atomic_add after a spin-lock on per-tile locks (nondeterministic summation order; spin-wait relies on scheduling assumptions). full_tiles casts to fp16 regardless of dtype. |
| layer_norm forward benchmark-only variants (xformers single block, tutorial multi-pass) | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.layer_norm(x, (N,), weight, bias, eps_eff) | yes | Docstrings: 'only used in benchmarks'. Both ignore HAS_BIAS/IS_RMSNORM and always load bias; with bias=None the wrapper passes the input tensor as bias pointer, so garbage is added. xformers variant needs N <= 4096 (single block). |
| layer_norm forward, single-pass Welford (IS_RMSNORM=False) | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.layer_norm(x, (N,), weight, bias, eps_eff) with eps_eff = max(eps, 1.6e-5) if x.dtype==fp16 else eps; also writes mean and rstd (fp32, biased var) | yes | Code-visible deviation: eps silently raised to 1.6e-5 for fp16 inputs. Chan/Welford block merge verified correct. Weight required. Inputs cast to fp16 under autocast; optimizer also converts LayerNorm weights to fp16 in place. Inference only (no backward despite save_for_backward). |
| experimental llama: fused rmsnorm + SwiGLU feed-forward, fp16 weights | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | xn = F.rms_norm(x, (K,), rms_w, eps=1e-6); F.silu(xn @ W1.T) * (xn @ W3.T)  (== ff_pytorch in the same file) | yes | No K mask (K % 64 == 0), M/N modulo wrap; module-level test code runs on import. |
| experimental misc: empty launch-overhead kernel | — | torch 组合 |  | no-op (writes nothing) | yes | Body is `pass`; nothing to audit. |
| flash attention forward (additive mask / causal) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | attention_reference in the same file: softmax(q@k^T*sm_scale + mask' [+ top-left causal -inf], -1) @ v, where kernel uses mask' = mask.masked_fill(mask == -inf, torch.finfo(mask.dtype).min) | yes | fp16/bf16 only (fp32 cast to fp16 by custom_fwd). Mask additive, broadcastable over batch/heads/rows; -inf replaced by dtype min (fully masked row -> mean of v instead of NaN; library reference gives NaN). Causal is top-left aligned. Fixed BLOCK 128, no autotune. BUG: N-padding mask uses n_range_offs < n_size instead of block_n_offs, so for n_size > 128 with n_size % 128 != 0, no mask and non-causal, padded keys (k=0) enter the softmax denominator -> output scaled down (hidden in tests because q,k = rand >= 0 make real scores dominate). |
| linear + bias + fused activation forward | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | act(F.linear(x, W, b)) with act per constexpr ACTIVATION: ''->identity, 'tanh'->torch.tanh, 'gelu'->F.gelu(approximate='none'), 'fast_gelu'->F.gelu(approximate='tanh'), 'relu'->F.relu; optional act_inputs = F.linear(x, W, b) | yes | Inference only (no backward). x 2D or 3D (flatten(0,1)); W [N,K] torch layout; fp32 accumulation; inputs cast to fp16 under autocast (custom_fwd cast_inputs=fp16). M/N tails handled by modulo wrap + store mask; act_inputs store unmasked but writes duplicate identical values. Whole library targets triton==2.0.0 (tl.libdevice, triton.ops.matmul_perf_model) - must be ported for Triton 3.x. README benchmarks were run on RTX 3090 (sm_86). |
| rms_norm forward via the layer-norm kernel (IS_RMSNORM=True) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | F.rms_norm(x, (N,), weight, eps_eff) (+ bias if HAS_BIAS); eps_eff as in layer_norm family | yes | Optimizer replaces the T5-style pattern (eps=1e-6) with this kernel; for fp16 the kernel raises eps to 1.6e-5, deviating from the replaced pattern. Stored mean is 0. |
| skinny attention part 1: split-KV partial blocks | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | attention_split_1_reference in the same file: per 128-key block j: blocks_j = exp(S_j - max_j) @ V_j (unnormalized, stored fp16), maximums_j = rowmax(S_j), sums_j = rowsum(exp(S_j - max_j)), S = q@k^T*sm_scale (+mask, +causal) | yes | Wired by optimizer only for q_len==1, k_len>50, no mask, non-causal. Intermediate layout [B,H,cdiv(N,128),M,D] fp16 + fp32 max/sum [B,H,n_div,M]. Same padded-key bug as flash attention (affects the wired path whenever k_len > 128 and k_len % 128 != 0). Fully masked block (causal) gives NaN (exp(-inf - -inf)). Library reference only valid when N divisible by n_div. |
| skinny attention part 2: merge of split-KV partials | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | L = maximums.max(2, keepdim); w = exp(maximums - L); out = (w[...,None] * blocks).sum(2) / (w * sums).sum(2)[...,None] | yes | Online merge over n_div partials; NaN partials from part 1 propagate. |
| vec-mat (single-query attention building block) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | s = vec.float() * scaler; s = torch.softmax(s, -1) if softmax_vec else s; s @ (matrix.transpose(-1,-2) if transpose_mat else matrix).float()  (vec [B,H,1,K]) | yes | Used by attention_vec_mat_forward (q@k^T then softmax(sm_scale*s)@v; no mask/causal); not wired into the optimizer, only tests. BUG: with softmax, padded lanes (K not pow2) are loaded as 0 and included in max/sum -> denominator inflated. scaler is a float constexpr (recompile per value). |
| experimental fp8 <-> fp16 conversion kernels | — | 需要定义 | F | which fp8 variant tl.float8e5 / tl.float8e4 denote in the pinned Triton (e4 bias/nv vs b15)；rounding mode of fp16->fp8 store (RTNE vs truncation)；overflow/saturation and NaN/inf mapping；processes untyped_storage size (non-contiguous tensors) | no | Nested @triton.jit kernels defined inside the wrappers; module-level self-test loop runs on import. f8_to_f16 stores twice (benign). |
| experimental llama: FP8 (e5m2 bit-pattern) weight / KV variants (USE_FP8=True) | 前向 | 需要定义 | F | fp8 format = Triton 2.x tl.float8e5 stored in int8 tensors (bias/NaN/inf encoding)；no scaling factors (direct cast)；dequant path fp8->fp32->fp16 per kernel；rounding/saturation when kernels store fp8 outputs (k/v cache in rms_matmul_rbe_qkv) | no | Same math as fp16 families after dequantization (w.view(torch.float8_e5m2).half() if formats match). Library tests use atol=10 / 0.5. |
| experimental llama: flash attention with start_position, fp16 KV | 前向 | 需要定义 | B | causal alignment with KV-cache offset: kernel masks keys n > m - start_position while its loop bound implies n <= m + start_position (decode needs n <= m + start_position)；handling of fully masked rows (NaN) | yes | For start_position=0 equals F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q,k,v,is_causal=c,scale=sm_scale). Suspected bug for start_position>0 (only 0 tested). Same offs_n-vs-block_n_offs key-padding issue as kernl attention. Not used by the experimental model (which computes scores in torch). |
| experimental llama: fused rmsnorm + QKV projection + RoPE on q,k, fp16 | 前向 | 需要定义 | M | RoPE conventions as in rbe_triton；k/v written into caller-provided KV-cache views at start_pos | yes | q = rope(rmsnorm(x)@Wq^T), k = rope(rmsnorm(x)@Wk^T), v = rmsnorm(x)@Wv^T; used by the experimental llama model when enabled. |
| experimental llama: fused rmsnorm + matmul (+ RoPE epilogue), fp16 weights | 前向 | 需要定义 | M | RoPE conventions as in rbe_triton (RBE_EPILOGUE=True) | yes | RBE_EPILOGUE=False variant is torch_composition F.rms_norm(x,(K,),rms_w,1e-6) @ W.T. rmsnorm folded: (x*rms_w)@W^T * rsqrt(mean(x^2)+eps). No K mask (K % 64 == 0 required), M/N modulo wrap. Epilogue re-reads its own output tile after tl.debug_barrier. |
| experimental llama: rotary embedding (RoPE, interleaved pairs) | 前向 | 需要定义 | M | pairing convention: interleaved (2i,2i+1) vs half-split；theta (kernel constexpr THETA=1e4)；head_dim for frequency index (hard-coded DIM=128, freq index = col % 128)；position = start_token_position + row index；cos/sin computed in fp32 on the fly vs precomputed table | yes | Also inlined as helper in rms_matmul_rbe epilogue. Applies over the flattened n_heads*head_dim row. Tolerance in library test atol=1e-1. |

## lightllm

https://github.com/ModelTC/lightllm，提交 `3d9415f44470`，许可 Apache-2.0；`@triton.jit` 函数 233 个，入口 kernel 216 个。

| 分族 | 方向 | 类别 | 规格类别 | 参照程序，或待定事项 | sm_86 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| add_in_place | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | input.add_(other, alpha=alpha) | yes | In place; both tensors contiguous, same numel; computed in input dtype (no fp32 upcast). |
| backend_validator_softmax | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.softmax(x, dim=-1) | yes | Self-test kernel used to validate the Triton backend; contiguous 2D fp32. |
| gelu_erf | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.gelu(x, approximate='none') | yes | 1/sqrt(2) hard-coded as 0.7071067811 (truncated); output cast to input dtype. |
| layernorm | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.layer_norm(x, (N,), weight, bias, eps) | yes | Same row stride assumed for X and Y; biased variance. |
| moe_sum_reduce | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | input.sum(dim=1)  # input [T, topk, H] -> out [T, H] | yes | fp32 accumulation, output cast to input dtype; no routed scaling factor inside. |
| rmsnorm | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.rms_norm(x, (N,), weight=weight, eps=eps)  # weight may be None | yes | Second pass loads X+cols ignoring x_stride1 (first pass uses it); wrapper views x as [-1,N] so stride1 is normally 1. |
| rmsnorm_vit | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.rms_norm(x, (N,), weight=w, eps=eps) | yes | fp32 statistics; optional rounding of x*rstd to input dtype before weight (HF Llama style) - irrelevant in fp64. |
| vocab_logits_transpose | — | torch 运算 |  | local_logits.t().contiguous() | yes | Only used for contiguous bf16 [V,B] with B>=16; followed by torch.topk (tie order unspecified, not part of kernel). |
| append_shared_expert_topk | — | torch 组合 |  | ids=cat([topk_ids, (start+arange(S)).expand(T,S)],1); w=cat([topk_w, torch.sigmoid(gate[:, :S].float()) if gate is not None else ones(T,S)],1) | yes | Shared experts fused as extra routed slots. |
| deepep_expanded_layout | — | torch 组合 |  | m_indices = repeat_interleave(arange(E), round_up(n_e, A)); padding_mask[r]=1 for rows >= n_e within expert e's aligned span; zero_padding: recv_x[pad]=0, recv_x_scale[pad]=0, recv_topk_weights[pad]=0; compact: meta[:,2:] = [[r,-1,...,-1] for r] | no | Docstring-specified. zero_padding writes into fp8 recv_x (needs sm89); the other two are dtype-agnostic. Alignment must match DeepEP dispatch. |
| deepep_weighted_gather_chunk | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | for r, k: s=meta[r,2+k]; if start<=s<end: out[r] += chunk[s-start]*weights[s]  (fp32 accumulate per call, stored in out dtype) | yes | Accumulator re-rounded to output dtype after each chunk call (multi-chunk results depend on chunking). |
| deepstack_embedding_add | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | for image i, rows s with st[i]<=id[s]<st[i]+len[i]: out[s] += deep[loc[i] + id[s] - st[i]] | yes | In place; image id ranges assumed disjoint. |
| destindex_copy_kv | — | torch 组合 |  | Out[DestLoc.long()] = K | yes | K [T,H,D], Out [S,H,D]; offs_d wraps mod head_dim when D not pow2 (duplicate identical writes, harmless). DestLoc assumed unique. |
| dflash2_greedy_selector_walk | — | torch 组合 |  | p=0; for d: s=lattice[r,d,p]; j=s.argmax() (first max); out[r,d]=cand[r,d,j]; conf[r,d] = -log(sum_{i!=j} exp(s_i - s_j)); p=j | yes | Ties resolved to lowest candidate index explicitly (matches torch.argmax first-max). |
| dflash2_grouped_dynamic_conv | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | for t,c,j<K: if t%block>=j: out[t,c] += hidden[t-j,c]*(base[side,j,c] + dyn[t, (side*K+j)*G + c//gs]) | yes | Causal conv restricted to each block_size draft block (docstring). |
| dsa_indexer_k_cache_copy | — | torch 组合 |  | copy: buf[dest, :, :128] = k_fp8 bytes, buf[dest, :, 128:132] = scale bytes; extract: concat over requests r (last row of each mtp group) of buf[req_to_token[req_r, :seq_r]] split into fp8/scale; topk map: torch.where(idx!=-1, ragged[idx.clamp(min=0)], -1) | no | Pure byte copies of a 132-byte fp8+fp32 per-token record; copy/extract kernels touch fp8-typed tensors (topk map is int-only and runs on sm86). topk width fixed at 2048. |
| embed_cache_offload | — | torch 组合 |  | e=embed if embed.dim()==3 else embed[:,None]; cache[s:s+T,:e.shape[1]]=e; cache[s:s+T,e.shape[1]:]=0 | yes | Cache is [token, layer(<=4), H] (Qwen3-VL deepstack); layers beyond the source are zero-filled on purpose. |
| flashmla_fp8_prefill_compact_kv | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | row_table[prefill_mem_index[i]] = i; for unique mem s: compact[s] = prefill_kv[row_table[mem]] if row_table[mem] != -1 else cat([fp8_nope[mem].view(G,gs)*scale[mem][:,None], rope[mem]]).float() | no | Fresh prefill rows take precedence over the dequantised cache; dequant formula fixed by the packed layout. |
| fp8_block_weight_dequant | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | (x.double() * s.repeat_interleave(B,0).repeat_interleave(B,1)[:M,:N]).to(torch.bfloat16) | no | Block size fixed by API (default 128) so no free choice; input is fp8 e4m3 weight (needs sm89+). Output via default dtype then bf16. |
| fp8_scaled_gemm | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | bmm: torch.bmm(a.double(), b.double()) * a_scale * b_scale.transpose(1,2)  (a_scale [B,M,1], b_scale [B,N,1]); block: (A.double()*As.repeat_interleave(gk,1)[:, :K]) @ (B.double()*Bs.repeat_interleave(gk,0)[:K].repeat_interleave(gn,1)[:, :N]), cast to out dtype | no | Consumers of already-quantised fp8 operands; scale granularity is fixed by the scale tensor shapes. Block GEMM requires BLOCK_K <= group_k. |
| gated_activation_and_mul | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | blocked: g,u = x[..., :N], x[..., N:]; interleaved: g,u = x[...,0::2], x[...,1::2]; default out = F.silu(g)*u; tanh mode / gemma: F.gelu(g, approximate='tanh')*u; limit/alpha (gpt-oss): g=g.clamp(max=limit); u=u.clamp(-limit,limit); out=(u+1 if clamp_up_add_one else u)*(g*torch.sigmoid(alpha*g)) | yes | Activation rounded to input dtype before the multiply (product in input dtype). Layout and options are explicit API flags. |
| gated_rmsnorm | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | act = sigmoid if gate_type=='sigmoid' else F.silu; xg = x.view(M,G,gs); if norm_before_gate: y = F.rms_norm(xg,(gs,),eps=eps)*w.view(G,gs)(+b) * act(z) else y = F.rms_norm(xg*act(z),(gs,),eps=eps)*w(+b) | yes | Group-wise norm (group_size); z may be a strided [tokens, heads, N] view. |
| gdn_gating | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | g = -torch.exp(A_log.float()) * F.softplus(a.float() + dt_bias.float(), beta=beta, threshold=threshold); beta_out = torch.sigmoid(b.float()) | yes | g stored in g dtype, beta in b dtype. |
| gemma4_image_token_end_builder | — | torch 组合 |  | out=zeros(sum_q); for each image (start,len) of batch b: rows q in [start,start+len) within this chunk get out[q_start[b]+pos-ready[b]] = start+len | yes | Bookkeeping for the bidirectional image mask. |
| glm5_kpool_gather_bookkeeping | — | torch 组合 |  | ranges: start=batch*MAX_POOLS, end=start+len//4, pool_len=len//4; gather: K[b*P+p] = packed[req_table[req_b, 4p+3], :128], scale = packed[...,128:132] (0/1.0 for invalid pools); paged: same rows packed 64 per DeepGEMM page as [64x128 fp8 \| 64 fp32 scales] | no | gather_pools bit-casts to float8e4nv (needs sm89); the other two are integer/byte-only. |
| gpu_atomic_event | — | torch 组合 |  | add_value: x.view(-1)[0] += 1 ; wait_value: no output, spins until x.view(-1)[0] == expected | yes | Cross-stream synchronisation primitive (acq_rel gpu-scope atomics); wait_value hangs forever if the value never matches. Only termination/ordering is checkable. |
| hadamard_transform_128 | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | (x.double() @ H128) * scale  where H128 = Sylvester Hadamard (scipy.linalg.hadamard(128), natural order) | yes | bf16 input, hidden size 128 only. |
| kv_buffer_slot_copy | — | torch 组合 |  | kv_move: buf[:, dst] = buf[:, src]; mamba copy: dst[:, dst_idx] = src[:, src_idx]; fork: dst[:, dst_idx] = src[:, src_idx.repeat_interleave(num_dst_per_src)]; tensor_copy_3dim: dest.copy_(src) | yes | In-place same-buffer copy (kv_move) is order-dependent if src/dst sets overlap; reference assumes disjoint. Byte-level (uint8 view) copies. |
| kv_state_cpu_offload | — | torch 组合 |  | offload: for page p with page_idx[p]!=-1 and not ready[p]: cpu[page_idx[p], l, t, cpu_heads] = gpu[l, tok_idx[p*TB+t], gpu_heads] (head lists from wrapper's TP mapping; same for scales); load is the inverse gather/scatter; hybrid/linear-att variants are byte-wise copies of conv/ssm state rows into cpu pages | yes | Writes/reads pinned host memory via UVA. Expected output fully fixed by wrapper code, but the TP head mapping (K/V halves, only tp_index%scale==0 writes) is a library layout to replicate. Kernel uses first head index + count, i.e. assumes contiguous head ranges. |

| sampling_penalties | — | 需要定义 | I | order: repetition (divide if >0 else multiply) then frequency (count*f) then presence；token set = output tokens only (counter) vs supplied p_token_ids；exponential-decay length penalty: eos += \|eos\|*(decay^len_param - 1)；eos masking value -1e7 | yes | Deterministic in-place logits edits; no sampling here. |

## Lightning Attention

https://github.com/OpenNLPLab/lightning-attention，提交 `d15c38529bbd`，许可 MIT；`@triton.jit` 函数 17 个，入口 kernel 16 个。

| 分族 | 方向 | 类别 | 规格类别 | 参照程序，或待定事项 | sm_86 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SimpleRMSNorm (no affine weight) | 前向+反向 | torch 运算 |  | F.rms_norm(x, (x.shape[-1],), weight=None, eps=eps); backward = autograd w.r.t. x | yes | Row-wise over last dim, N*elem_size < 64KB. Deviation: for fp16 inputs eps is silently raised to max(eps,1.6e-5). rstd saved in fp32. bwd loads x/dy in native dtype (promoted by fp32 rstd). Output stored with input row stride. |
| causal linear attention without decay (lightning attention-2, s=None) | 前向+反向 | torch 组合 |  | o = torch.tril(q @ k.transpose(-1,-2)) @ v  (== linear_attn(q,k,v,None)); backward = autograd w.r.t. q,k,v | yes | Same layout/domain constraints as the decay variant (d power of 2 >=16, e multiple of block, contiguous). No scaling, no normalisation, inclusive diagonal. TF32 dot on fp32-upcast inputs. |
| lightning attention-2 with decay, fully-parallel variant | 前向+反向 | 需要定义 | A | same decay/normalisation decisions as the chunk-loop variant；input domain: n must be a multiple of 256 (NUM_BLOCK = n//256; kv/none-diag kernels unmasked, tail rows of torch.empty output never written) | yes | Selected via lightning_attn_func(..., variant='parallel'). Per-block KV states stored in fp32 (b,h,n/256,d,e) and prefix-reduced with block_decay=exp(-256*s). No repo test covers this variant. Duplicate definition of _fwd_none_diag_kernel: only the second (CBLOCK-tiled) one is used. TF32 dot on fp32-upcast inputs. |
| lightning attention-2 with per-head exponential decay (chunk-loop variant) | 前向+反向 | 需要定义 | A | decay parameterisation: kernel uses exp(-s_h*(i-j)) with s = per-head slope (comment calls it 'log lambda'; actual lambda = exp(-s))；no softmax, no 1/sqrt(d) scale, no normaliser/denominator；s shape (h,1,1) fp32 shared across batch, non-differentiable；causal (j<=i) inclusive diagonal；whether to follow torch ref's intermediate cast of (qk*M) to q.dtype (float64 ref drops it) | yes | Layout (b,h,n,d)/(b,h,n,e) contiguous; any n (masked); d must be a power of 2 >=16 (tl.arange(0,d), no d-mask) and e a multiple of min(next_pow2(e),32) (interface pads e). Inputs upcast to fp32 and tl.dot on fp32 operands -> TF32 by default on Ampere (precision deviation). Intra/inter block split is algebraically exact (decay exp(-s*(BLOCK-j))*exp(-s*i)). bwd: intra kernel writes, inter kernel read-modify-writes dq/dk/dv in output dtype (extra rounding). Interface bug: is_support(dim)=16 % dim rejects d or e in {1,2,4,8,16}. Large d/e fp32 tiles may stress A6000's ~99KB smem. |

## LMDeploy

https://github.com/InternLM/lmdeploy，提交 `822dddb8b3bc`，许可 Apache-2.0；`@triton.jit` 函数 142 个，入口 kernel 89 个。

| 分族 | 方向 | 类别 | 规格类别 | 参照程序，或待定事项 | sm_86 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| env_check_vector_add | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | c = a + b | yes | Triton install sanity check, not a model op. domain: 1-D contiguous a,b of equal length (size = a.size(0)). |
| copy_cache_blocks | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | lc = cache.unflatten(entry_axis, (-1, pages_per_block)); lc.index_copy_(entry_axis, dst_blocks, lc.index_select(entry_axis, src_blocks)) | yes | Byte-level copy of logical blocks (pages_per_block pages) inside one contiguous pool. domain: src and dst block sets disjoint (overlap is a race). |
| cuda_graph_common_inputs | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | candidate (lib ref): CPU branch of fill_graph_common_inputs (copy ids/positions prefix, zero-pad block table, qkv_lens rows [q_start, q_len, kv_len] padded with pad_query_len, cu_seqlens = exclusive cumsum, DCP local kv lens) | yes | Graph input padding bookkeeping; reference already in-library. |
| dcp_attention_state_merge | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | sanitize: torch.where((counts>0)[:,None] & ~isnan(lse) & (lse!=inf), lse.float(), -inf); correct: w = softmax(lse_g, dim=0)[rank] (0 if all -inf); out = (local*w[...,None]).transpose(0,1); merge: L = logaddexp(lp, ls); out = po*exp(lp-L) + so*exp(ls-L) (0 and -inf when both -inf) | yes | fp32 arithmetic, output keeps input dtype. NaN outputs with zero weight are forced to 0. |
| dcp_index_compaction | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | decode: valid = (idx>=0)&(idx%S==R); vals = torch.where(valid, idx//S, -1); prefill: valid=(idx>=start)&(idx<start+len); vals = torch.where(valid, idx-start+base, -1); order = torch.argsort((~valid).int(), dim=-1, stable=True); out = vals.gather(-1, order); counts = valid.sum(-1) | yes | Stable compaction with -1 padding; int32. |
| dcp_prefill_kv_reorder | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | for row (rank r, local p) of rank-major gathered [S*cap, W]: find request q with cum(local_lens[r])[q-1] <= p < cum[q]; g = (p - start)*S + r; if g < chunk_kv_seqlens[q]: output[kv_start_loc[q] + g] = gathered[row] | yes | Padding rows of output left untouched. |
| dcp_topk_candidate_pack | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | valid = local_idx>=0; s = scores.gather(1, local_idx.clamp(0, W-1)).masked_fill(~valid, -inf); gid = torch.where(valid, local_idx.clamp(0,W-1)*S + R, -1).int(); packed = torch.stack([s, gid.view(torch.float32)], -1) | yes | Id lane is int32 bit pattern stored in fp32 storage (bitcast, not numeric). The companion global top-k is TileLang (out of scope). |
| dsa_indexer_metadata | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | vis = kv_len[b] - q_len[b] + i + 1 for i < q_len[b]; indexer_kv_seqlens[cu_q[b]+i] = clamp((vis + S-1-R)//S, min=0); expanded_block_offsets[cu_q[b]+i, :] = block_offsets[b, :] | yes | Bookkeeping fixed by docstring (global causal length -> DCP rank-local). device_assert compiled out without TRITON_DEBUG. |
| dsv4_hyper_connection_pre_post | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | pre: out = torch.einsum('nh,nhd->nd', pre, x).to(out_dtype); post: out = post[...,None]*x[:,None,:] + torch.einsum('nso,nsd->nod', comb, residual) | yes | comb is [n, src, out]; fp32 accumulation, output in x dtype (pre: out_dtype). |
| ep_token_gather | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | out[t] = sum_k [ids[t,k] >= 0] * w[t,k] * inp[index[t,k]] | yes | domain: hidden % 1024 == 0. deviation: accumulation done in output dtype (bf16), not fp32 (comment: align with xtuner). |
| fused_multi_lora | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | for seq b with adapter a=adapter_ids[b], rows s=seq_start[b]..+seq_lens[b], R=slice(rank_start[a], rank_start[a]+ranks[a]): y = ((x[s] @ lora_a[R].T).to(lora_b.dtype) * scaling[a]) @ lora_b[R]; out[s] = y (or out[s] += y when cum) | yes | rank 0 adapters write zeros (non-cum). deviation: intermediate x@A rounded to lora_b dtype before scaling; bf16 cum path uses load+add+store (safe: tiles not shared). |
| gated_delta_preprocess | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | q2 = q.float()/torch.sqrt((q.float()**2).sum(-1, keepdim=True)+1e-6) (same for k, if apply_qk_l2norm); q2 = q2.repeat_interleave(kv_ratio, -2); beta = sigmoid(b); g = a_log_exp * F.softplus(a + dt_bias, threshold=20); g[init_token_mask] = -1e6 | yes | Head mapping dst = src*kv_ratio + rep (repeat_interleave). Outputs: q/k in q dtype, beta in b dtype, g fp32. |
| gated_delta_state_select_scatter | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | select: out = state[state_indices, spec_offsets]; scatter: state[state_indices, spec_offsets] = src.to(state.dtype) | yes | domain: inner dims contiguous; duplicate (index, offset) pairs in scatter are a race. |
| moe_grouped_gemm_bf16 | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | for each local expert e and sorted position p in [exp_start[e+off], exp_end[e+off]): r = sorted_idx[p]; a = r//top_k if reindex_a else p; c = r if reindex_c else p; C[c] = A[a] @ B[e].T (+ bias[e]) | yes | Depends on the routing metadata format (moe_route_sort_metadata) but output is fixed once metadata is given. domain: B [E,N,K] row-major per expert; rows of C for non-local routes untouched (pre-zeroed when EP). deviation: fp32 acc rounded to A dtype. Compact variant only chosen on sm90+ by the wrapper (kernel itself plain). |
| moe_weighted_reduce | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | out = (h * w[..., None]).sum(1)  # h [M, top_k, N], w [M, top_k] | yes | deviation: without fp32_acc the product and sum are done in hidden dtype. |
| rejection_sampling_greedy | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | accept draft tokens while draft==target; out[:, :n_acc] = target[:, :n_acc] (first mismatch position takes target), bonus appended if all accepted, rest = PLACEHOLDER_TOKEN_ID; num_rejected = S+1-n_out; last = last written token | yes | Rows with is_greedy==False skipped (left for random kernel). |
| rejection_sampling_random | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | accept draft d at pos i iff q_d>0 and p_d/q_d >= u[i] (q=1 without draft_probs); on first reject emit recovered[i] = argmax_v(max(p-q,0)[v]*inv_q[v]) (no draft probs: p with draft token zeroed); then PLACEHOLDER; bonus if none rejected | yes | Randomness (uniform_probs fp64, inv_q = 1/Exponential) is generated by the caller (backends/cuda/rejection_sampling.py), so kernels are deterministic given those inputs. argmax ties -> first index (tl.max tie_break_left, strict > across blocks), same as torch.argmax. |
| rms_norm_residual | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | r = x + residual if residual is not None else x; out = torch.nn.functional.rms_norm(r, (N,), weight, eps); return (out, r) if residual is not None else out | yes | domain: x 1-3D, last dim N == weight.numel(), weight contiguous. deviation: normalisation done in fp32 and rounded to input dtype BEFORE multiplying by weight (HF LlamaRMSNorm order); residual sum rounded to input dtype before normalising. |
| rotary_embedding_qk | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | rotate_half mode (cos/sin [S,R]): xr = x[..., :R]; out[..., :R] = xr*cos[:,None] + torch.cat([-xr[..., R//2:], xr[..., :R//2]], -1)*sin[:,None]; complex mode (cos/sin [S,R/2]): xe, xo = xr[..., 0::2], xr[..., 1::2]; out_e = xe*cos[:,None] - xo*sin[:,None]; out_o = xo*cos[:,None] + xe*sin[:,None]; same for k | yes | domain: q,k [S,H,D] with D >= rope dim R; cos/sin already gathered per token. deviation: when D > R and q_embed/k_embed are not passed, dims R..D of the outputs are left uninitialised (torch.empty_like) instead of copied; cos/sin rounded to q dtype first; arithmetic written to avoid FMA. |
| silu_and_mul | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | N = x.shape[-1]//2; out = F.silu(x[..., :N]) * x[..., N:]  # EP variant: x [E,M,2N], only rows m < min(mask_m[e], M) are written | yes | Gate is the first half, up the second half. deviation: silu evaluated in fp32 then rounded to input dtype before the multiply. EP variant leaves rows >= mask_m[e] of the (torch.empty) output uninitialised. |
| symm_mem_all_gather | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | out = torch.cat([x_rank0, ..., x_rank{W-1}], dim=-1)  # [T, H/W] shards -> [T, H]; barrier kernel has no data output | no | Multi-GPU only: symmetric memory + multimem.st (NVLink SHARP multicast, sm90+), inline PTX signal/barrier ops. bf16 only, 16-byte alignment. |

| moe_grouped_gemm_w8a8 | 前向 | 需要定义 | H | per-token activation scale [M,1] x per-output-channel weight scale [E,N,1]；int32 accumulation (int8) or fp32 (fp8), scales applied after accumulation in fp32；activation quantisation done upstream (per_token_quant_int8 / static fp8)；same route metadata conventions as moe_grouped_gemm_bf16 | yes | sm86: int8 path plain (int8 mma); static fp8 path needs cc>=8.9. |
| moe_route_sort_metadata | 前向 | 需要定义 | H | order of routes within an expert segment (atomic counters -> nondeterministic)；route id r = token*top_k + k (flattened topk_ids)；compact block metadata format (block_end cumsum, block_expert_ids local ids, block_offsets)；expert_offset/local experts for EP | yes | Deterministic parts: exp_start/exp_end = exclusive/inclusive cumsum of bincount(topk_ids); sorted_idx must be a permutation grouping routes by expert. Check per-segment set equality rather than order. Single-CTA path selected only on sm90. |
| moe_router_noaux_tc | 前向 | 需要定义 | H | top-k tie-breaking (repeated argmax -> lowest index first)；output order descending by biased score (HF topk(sorted=False) unspecified)；group score = sum of top-2 biased scores; top-2 found by removing first argmax only；non-selected groups masked to 0.0 (HF) not -inf (DeepSeek official)；weights from unbiased sigmoid scores, renorm eps 1e-20, routed_scaling_factor；single-group: inputs already scores (no sigmoid), always renormalised | yes | single-group router hard-asserts num_experts==192, top_k==8. |
| paged_decode_attention | 前向 | 需要定义 | B | paged KV layout (bshd or bhsd; page_table[b,i] = physical block of logical block i, block size = page size)；multi-token decode: seq_len = num_tokens/batch query tokens per request all attend to all cache_seqlens keys (no intra-step causal mask)；sliding window key j visible iff j >= kv_len-1-window；alibi bias -slope*\|kv_len-j\|；sinks added in split-K reduce denominator；softcap；GQA head mapping；split-K partitioning and 1e-10 denominator epsilon；shared k/v buffer (MLA) | yes | Two-stage split-K: stage 1 writes (acc, m, l) to fp32 scratch, stage 2 merges. PDL (gdc_wait/launch_dependents) only enabled on sm90+. deviation: requests with cache_seqlens <= 0 return early without writing scratch, so the reduce reads uninitialised memory (undefined output). |
| paged_decode_attention_quant_kv | 前向 | 需要定义 | B | int8/int4 asymmetric per-token-per-head dequant (x_q - zero)*scale；int4 packing: dims d and d+D/2 share a byte (low nibble = first half)；fp8 per-tensor scales applied to QK^T and to P before PV；TurboQuant: q Hadamard-rotated (normalised Walsh-Hadamard, natural order), K = mse_norm*(centroid3[idx] + qjl_norm*sign), V = norm*centroid2[idx], output inverse-rotated in reduce；Lloyd-Max codebook values and sigma scaling；same decode semantics as paged_decode_attention | yes | Reduce for int/fp8 policies is _reduce_split_kernel (paged_decode_attention family). sm86: int4/int8/TurboQuant and fp8_e5m2 paths are plain; fp8_e4m3 KV needs cc>=8.9 (lib tests skip e4m3 below cc 9.0). deviation: the quant kernel indexes q by batch id and ignores seq_len, so >1 query token per request gives wrong/uninitialised rows (wrapper does not assert). possible deviation (static reading, unverified): TurboQuant K centroid sigma in-kernel is 1/sqrt(2*D_raw) while fill/flatten/test codebooks use 1/sqrt(D_raw). _fused_reduce_hadamard_kernel uses inline PTX bar.sync and atomic_xchg scratch for the butterfly. |
| per_token_quant | 前向 | 需要定义 | F | scale = max(amax, eps)/qmax per row；rounding: libdevice.round (half away from zero) vs torch.round (half to even) in the lib reference；fp8 output: no rounding step, cast at store (RTNE)；no clamp | yes | sm86: int8 path plain; fp8 output needs cc>=8.9. deviation: kernel rounds half away from zero while the in-file torch reference uses round-half-even. |
| rms_norm_dynamic_quant | 前向 | 需要定义 | F | rms norm output rounded to input dtype before quant；scale = amax/qmax per row with no eps guard (all-zero row -> nan)；round half away from zero then clamp [qmin,qmax]；residual sum stored in input dtype | yes | sm86: int8 path plain; fp8 path needs cc>=8.9. |
| varlen_flash_attention_prefill | 前向 | 需要定义 | B | varlen packing (cu_seqlens_q/k or start_loc+seqlens)；causal mask bottom-right aligned (query i sees keys j <= kv_len-q_len+i)；sliding window: left bound only (j >= kv_len-q_len+i-window), window_size[1] ignored；block_sparse_size: causal mask on j//bs*bs；alibi bias -slope_h*\|j-(kv_len-q_len+i)\|；attention sinks: exp(sink_h) added to softmax denominator；softcap tanh applied before masking；GQA mapping kv_head = h // (Hq/Hkv); kv_layout shd/hsd；LSE output in natural log, fp32；fully-masked / empty rows behaviour | yes | BLOCK_DK split into pow2 + remainder chunk for head_dim like 576; shared_kv (MLA, v = k[..., :Dv]) when k and v share storage. domain: head_dim_q == head_dim_k. deviation: masked logits use -1e30 (not -inf). |
| w8a8_linear | 前向 | 需要定义 | G | out = (A_q @ B_q.T) * a_scale[m] * w_scale[n] (int32 or fp32 accumulation, scales fp32)；residual added after rounding to residual dtype；bias added outside kernel；static fp8 per-tensor input scale broadcast | yes | sm86: int8 path plain; fp8 inputs need cc>=8.9. deviation: K tail loads use other=None and M/N use modulo wrap (rows masked on store). |
| multinomial_sampling_sorted | 前向 | 随机 | R | Philox stream (tl.rand(seed, offset)) per row；inverse-CDF selection: first i with cum[i-1] < u <= cum[i]；fallback to indices[b,0] when u exceeds total mass | yes | Deterministic checks: one-hot score rows must return the indexed token exactly; output always an element of indices[b]; for a fixed u the inverse-CDF choice is checkable; empirical frequencies vs scores. domain: scores sorted descending and normalised. |

## SageAttention (Triton parts)

https://github.com/thu-ml/SageAttention，提交 `d1a57a546c3d`，许可 Apache-2.0；`@triton.jit` 函数 15 个，入口 kernel 9 个。

| 分族 | 方向 | 类别 | 规格类别 | 参照程序，或待定事项 | sm_86 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SageAttention3 per-128-row group mean subtraction of Q | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | qm = q.view(B,H,L//128,128,D).mean(-2); q_out = q - qm.repeat_interleave(128, dim=2)  (outputs q_out, qm in q.dtype) | yes | L must be a multiple of 128 and D a power of 2 (no masking; preprocess_qkv zero-pads L to 128, so the last group's mean includes padding rows -- a caller convention). Reduction done by tl.sum on the input dtype, result stored in q.dtype. The kernel itself is plain Triton, but the module imports fp4attn_cuda/fp4quant_cuda (Blackwell-only extensions), so it cannot be imported on sm86 without stubbing. |
| INT8-QK / FP16-PV attention on pre-quantised inputs, causal (dense) | 前向 | 需要定义 | B | reference target (dequantised-input exact vs. unquantised SDPA with tolerance)；exp2 domain with sm_scale*log2(e) folded into Q；fp16 P and fp16 per-block PV accumulation；LSE log2 units / smooth-K correction done outside；requires qo_len == kv_len (asserted), so top-left == bottom-right causal | yes | Uses -inf masking (no fully-masked rows possible with equal lengths). No attn_mask support (core.py asserts). |
| INT8-QK / FP16-PV attention on pre-quantised inputs, non-causal, optional mask + LSE (dense) | 前向 | 需要定义 | B | reference target: exact attention on dequantised int8 inputs vs. SDPA on original fp16 q,k,v with an error budget；float attn_mask is added in the log2 (exp2) domain, i.e. equals SDPA mask*ln2 -- differs from SDPA attn_mask semantics；bool mask: masked scores get -1e6 (not -inf); key blocks whose mask is all False are skipped；fully-masked rows: l_i initialised to 1.0 -> output 0 if every block skipped, otherwise uniform average over masked keys of non-skipped blocks (SDPA gives NaN)；P cast to fp16 and each P@V block accumulated with out_dtype=fp16 before fp32 accumulation; V must be fp16 (bf16 converted by core.py)；LSE returned in log2 units and without the smooth-K correction (core.py rescales by 1/1.44269504 and adds q.km*sm_scale)；GQA head mapping h_kv = h // (H/H_kv) | yes | head_dim 64 or 128 (core.py pads), fp16/bf16 output. int8 x int8 tl.dot (int32 acc) is supported on sm80/sm86. Smooth-K does not change softmax output (row-constant shift) but changes LSE. |
| INT8-QK / FP16-PV attention on pre-quantised inputs, varlen (causal and non-causal) | 前向 | 需要定义 | B | varlen packing conventions (cu_seqlens_q/k, per-sequence scale offsets cu_seqlens_*_scale)；causal alignment per sequence is top-left (offs_m >= offs_n); docstring requires qo_len == kv_len per sequence but nothing enforces it；sequences with kv_len==0 produce 0 output (l_i init 1.0)；same quantised-reference / exp2 / fp16-PV decisions as the dense kernels；K smoothing uses global mean across sequences；no LSE output | yes | Layout (total_tokens, H, D); GQA supported. |
| per-block INT8 quantisation of Q and K (dense) | 前向 | 需要定义 | F | block granularity: BLKQ=128 rows x full head_dim per (b,h) for Q, BLKK=64 for K；rounding = round-half-away-from-zero via +0.5*sign then truncating cast；sm_scale*log2(e) folded into Q before quantisation (exp2-domain attention)；K smoothing km = k.mean over sequence per (b,h_kv), applied before quant (core.py smooth_k)；all-zero block: scale=0 -> 0/0 NaN cast to int8 (no epsilon, unlike per-thread)；tail-block rows loaded with mask but no `other` (formally undefined values enter the absmax)；scale tensor layout (b,h,ceil(L/BLK)) fp32; HND/NHD layouts | yes | Inputs fp16/bf16 (padded to head_dim 64 or 128 by core.py). Used by sageattn_qk_int8_pv_fp16_triton (quantization_backend='triton'). |
| per-block INT8 quantisation of Q and K (varlen) | 前向 | 需要定义 | F | same quantisation decisions as dense per-block；varlen packing: blocks restart per sequence; scale layout (sum_b ceil(L_b/BLK), H) indexed via cu_seqlens_scale；K smoothing in sageattn_varlen uses the mean over ALL packed tokens of all sequences (not per sequence) | yes | Layout (total_tokens, H, D). Programs with off_blk*BLK >= L_b exit early. |
| per-thread INT8 quantisation of Q and K (for CUDA mma attention kernels) | 前向 | 需要定义 | F | thread-group membership tied to mma.m16n8k32 fragment layout (Q: 8 groups per warp segment, K: 4 groups of row pairs)；WARPQ/BLKQ/WARPK/BLKK chosen by caller per GPU arch and head_dim (16/32/64/128)；epsilon 1e-7 added to scale；no sm_scale folding here (sm_scale argument is computed but unused; CUDA attention kernel applies it)；K smoothing km supplied by caller；scale layout (b,h,nblk*(BLK/WARP)*8 or *4) | yes | Triton producer for CUDA attention kernels (sm80/sm89/sm90 builds). The int4 variants in the same file are dead code (never launched). |

## sglang

https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang，提交 `a57707377f3c`，许可 Apache-2.0；`@triton.jit` 函数 956 个，入口 kernel 805 个。

| 分族 | 方向 | 类别 | 规格类别 | 参照程序，或待定事项 | sm_86 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| batch_invariant_log_softmax | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.log_softmax(x, dim=-1) | yes | Last dim only; arithmetic in input dtype (no explicit fp32 upcast); _half_to_float=True asserts (not implemented). |
| batch_invariant_matmul | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.addmm(bias, a, b) if bias is not None else a @ b ; bmm: torch.bmm(a, b) | yes | aten mm/addmm/bmm override; fp32 accumulation, fixed tile configs for bf16/fp16/fp32 (persistent, batch-invariant reduction order); output cast to out dtype (fp8 out branch exists but configs only for 16/32-bit inputs). 1D bias only. |
| batch_invariant_mean | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.mean(x, dim=d, keepdim=keepdim) | yes | Single-dim kernel; integer inputs cast to fp32; mean_batch_invariant ignores dtype=float32 for the single-dim path (output stays input dtype). Multi-dim path is plain torch. |
| batch_invariant_rms_norm | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.rms_norm(x, (x.shape[-1],), weight, eps) | yes | fp32 math, output in input dtype; aten compat uses eps=finfo(dtype).eps when None (matches torch); weight None -> ones. |
| dsv4_wo_a_bf16_gemm | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.einsum('tgd,grd->tgr', x.float(), weight.float()).to(torch.bfloat16) | yes | Hard-wired shapes: gemv T=1 with pow2 D; small-batch 2<=T<=8, G=2, R=1024, D=4096; fixed 8-way split-K fp32 partials. |
| gemma_qk_rmsnorm_with_optional_gate | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.rms_norm(q.reshape(-1,hd), (hd,), 1+q_weight, eps), F.rms_norm(k.reshape(-1,hd), (hd,), 1+k_weight, eps); gate variant: q_gate.view(T,H,2hd) -> q=[..., :hd] normed, gate=[..., hd:] copied | yes | Gemma convention weight+1. Output always rounded to bf16 unless fp16 (fp32 inputs get bf16-precision results). Assumes k_rows <= q_rows. |
| mimo_local_audio_attention | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q,k,v,is_causal=is_causal,scale=scale).transpose(1,2)  # q,k,v [B,H,T<=4,16] | yes | Only T<=4, D=16, bf16/fp16. Deviation: unnormalized exp weights rounded to input dtype before PV (mirrors cuDNN). |
| mla_qk_rmsnorm_fallback | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.nn.functional.rms_norm(x, (N,), weight, eps) applied independently to q (q_weight,q_eps) and k (k_weight,k_eps) | yes | fp32 math, cast to input dtype. Used on gfx1250 when aiter fused kernel unavailable. |
| nearest_upsample_nhwc | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.interpolate(x, scale_factor=(fh, fw), mode='nearest')  # identical to 'nearest-exact' for integer factors | yes | Integer factors only; dense channels_last input with C>1; output channels_last; pure gather. |
| plain_rmsnorm | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.rms_norm(x.float(), (D,), None if w is None else w.float(), eps).to(x.dtype) | yes | rms_normalize (DSV4 HIP compressor) runs in place with optional weight; rmsnorm_fp32 uses enable_fp_fusion=False. |
| qk_head_layernorm | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.layer_norm(q,(D,),eps=eps), F.layer_norm(k,(D,),eps=eps)  # no affine, q/k [B,S,H,D] bf16 | yes | Bit-exact emulation of aten LN reduction for D<=128, D%4==0. Output fp32 math stored bf16. |
| rmsnorm | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.rms_norm(x, (D,), weight, eps)  # eps fixed 1e-6 for cast_before_weight; _square_* kernels emit x.float()**2 consumed by a torch mean | yes | Variants replicate different eager rounding: normalized value rounded to x.dtype before *weight (preserve_reduction, MOSS cast_before_weight, Z-Image), aten/Reduce.cuh reduction order emulation (bit-exact, width 128/3072, >=16 rows), Z-Image bf16 squares and bf16 rsqrt. Z-Image: bf16, head_dim 128 strided views. |
| router_gate_matvec | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.nn.functional.linear(x.float(), w.float())  # fp32 [M,E] logits | yes | Small-M (<=8) path; fp32 accumulation, deterministic. Output assumed contiguous (stride E). |
| router_logits_gemv | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.linear(x.float(), w.float())  (fp32 out; hip variant returns [K/512,M,N] partials whose ordered sum is this) | yes | Plain Triton but dispatch gated to gfx950. Split-K with self-cleaning atomic counter (last CTA reduces); bf16 inputs, N%BLOCK_N==0, K%512==0, M<=64. |
| spec.row_argmax | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.argmax(x, dim=-1) (div_argmax: torch.argmax(x / divisor, dim=-1), fp32 div_rn) | yes | fp32, unit column stride. Medium path (>64 rows or div) reproduces torch NaN-first; small-row path (<=64 rows) ignores NaN and returns N (out of range) for an all-NaN row - deviation; opt-in only (fused_argmax). |
| stable_topk | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | v,i=torch.sort(x,dim=-1,descending=True,stable=True); (v[:,:k], i[:,:k].int()) | yes | Lowest index wins ties (stable). Key packing orders -0.0 below +0.0 (torch treats equal) and +NaN first. k<=32, x contiguous 2-D. |
| adaln_fuse_scale_shift | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | x * (scale_constant + scale_b) + shift_b, scale/shift broadcast from scalar, [B,C], [B,L,C] or per-frame [B,F,1,C] (see fuse_scale_shift_kernel_native); scale_constant=None: x * scale + shift | yes | Arithmetic in input dtype (no fp32 upcast). 4D path: scale per frame, shift per token; a 4D shift is only expanded on HIP/XPU, on CUDA reshape(rows,C) of a [B,F,1,C] shift would fail. Both scalar zero => copy fast path. gfx1250 bf16 routes to torch native. |
| adaln_table_plus_temb_slices | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | ltx2: (table.to(temb.dtype)[None,None] + temb.view(B,S,9,D)).unbind(2); wan: (table[0][None,None] + temb.float()).permute(2,0,1,3)  # (6,B,S,D) fp32 | yes | LTX2: bf16 timestep, fp32/bf16 table cast to bf16 before the add (matches eager), D%256==0, D<=8192. | aot_ref_merge_attn_state | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | pl,sl = (where(l==inf,-inf,l) for l in (p_lse,s_lse)); m=maximum(pl,sl); a=exp(pl-m); b=exp(sl-m); out=(p_out*a[...,None]+s_out*b[...,None])/(a+b)[...,None]; lse=log(a+b)+m | yes | Natural-log LSE; +inf lse treated as -inf; both -inf gives NaN (0/0) in kernel and reference. Contiguous [T,H,D]/[T,H]. |
| aot_ref_moe_sum_reduce | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | (x.double().sum(dim=1) * routed_scaling_factor).to(x.dtype)  # x [T, topk, H] | yes | fp32 accumulation over topk, single rounding to input dtype; contiguous input/output. |
| append_shared_experts | — | torch 组合 |  | append: ids_o=cat([ids, N+arange(S)],1); w_o=cat([w, full((M,S),scale)],1); rows>=n_valid: routed ids=0, all weights 0; deepep: ids'=ids+(ids//nlr)*S, rows>=n_valid routed ids=pad_fill_id (weights kept), shared ids=shared_id_base+arange(S); with_weights: shared=sigmoid(h.float()@wg.float())*scale \| sigmoid(sw.float())*scale \| sw; fill_padded_rows: x[n_valid:]=fill_value | yes | Padded-row handling differs between append (zero weights) and deepep-remap (weights kept) kernels. |
| attention_page_table_metadata_builders | — | torch 组合 |  | cache_seqlens=(seq_lens+delta).int(); cu_seqlens_k=pad(cumsum(cache_seqlens),(1,0)); n=cdiv(cache_seqlens,ps); page_table[b,:n]=req_to_token[req_pool_indices[b], ::ps][:n]//ps; swa_page_table via full_to_swa_mapping[...]//ps; draft-extend adds cu_seqlens_q=pad(cumsum(extend_lens)); swa spec table row j=cat(a[j//R,:len_a], b[j,:len_b]); PA table row=req_to_token[r, cat(arange(pf), arange(max(pf,sl-W),sl))]; unified indices row=cat(prefix_idx, extend_idx); aligned window indices=left-pad with first key (pad=window_start%128) | yes | Only the live prefix of each page-table row is written; tails are stale across CUDA-graph replays, so compare live region only. page_size must be a power of 2. |
| causal_conv3d_cat_pad | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | out = F.pad(torch.cat([cache_x, x], dim=2), (pw, pw, ph, ph, pt_front - cache_t, 0)); channels_last_3d variant: out.contiguous(memory_format=torch.channels_last_3d), keep = out[:, :, -keep_t:, ph:ph+H, pw:pw+W] | yes | Pure data movement (zero fill); requires symmetric H/W padding, no back temporal padding, pt_front >= cache_t. cl3d variant returns None for unsupported inputs; the two cl3d kernels are one fn jitted with different specialization. |
| channel_l2norm_rmsnorm_wan_vae | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | wan: F.silu(F.normalize(x + conv_bias.view(1,C,1,1,1) if given, dim=1, eps=eps) * rms_scale * gamma + bias); finish: F.normalize(x, dim=1, eps=1e-12) * scale * weight | yes | Wan: channels_last_3d 5D, C<=1024, rms_scale default sqrt(C), output dtype promote(x,gamma). Assumes Lv==Lk (uses Lk for V width). Rows outside sequences untouched. |

| deepep_v2_psum_repack | — | torch 组合 |  | start[e]=align_up(psum[e-1],A) (0 for e=0); cnt[e]=min(psum[e]-start[e],MAX_M); expand->slab: out[e,j]=x[start[e]+j], scale_out[e,j,:]=s[start[e]+j] (MN-major), masked_m=cnt, overflow flag if raw>MAX_M; slab->expand: out[start[e]+j]=(y[e,j].float()*w[start[e]+j]).to(dtype) for j<cnt; fill_m_indices: m[start[e]:align_up(psum[e],A)]=e (rest -1); psum_init: start=psum[e-1], m[psum[e-1]:psum[e]]=e | yes | DeepEP v2 psum convention (inclusive, previous segment aligned). Padding rows left unwritten. |
| dequant_fp8_block128_upscale | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | out[m]=(a[m].float()*s[m].repeat_interleave(128)[:N]).to(out_dtype) for m<recv_token_num (other rows untouched) | no | Input is fp8 (DeepEP recv); ROCm aiter runner. |
