## stk

https://github.com/stanford-futuredata/stk，提交 `736313768ef6`，许可 Apache-2.0；`@triton.jit` 函数 4 个，入口 kernel 4 个。

| 分族 | 方向 | 类别 | 规格类别 | 参照程序，或待定事项 | sm_86 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| bcsr_row_indices (CSR offsets -> per-block row index) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | torch.repeat_interleave(torch.arange(offsets.numel()-1), torch.diff(offsets)).to(column_indices.dtype) | yes | grid=(block_rows,); integer bookkeeping, exact. domain: offsets monotone non-decreasing int32 with offsets[-1]==numel(out); out int16 (column_indices dtype), so block_rows must fit int16. |
| block_sparse_matmul_dds (dense x BCSR-sparse -> dense) | 前向+反向 | 需要定义 | K | BCSR format decode；column-wise traversal uses column_indices_t/offsets_t/block_offsets_t when B not transposed, row metadata when transposed；metadata consistency and duplicate handling (not validated)；block size 128 only；dense-operand transpose inferred from strides | yes | grid=(M/128, N/128); pid_n indexes a block column of B. Used in backward of SDD/DSD. domain/deviation as dsd (multiples of 128, no masks, fp32 via TF32, both-strides>1 heuristic on lhs). |
| block_sparse_matmul_dsd (BCSR-sparse x dense -> dense) | 前向+反向 | 需要定义 | K | BCSR format decode (to_dense: block i placed at rows r_i*bs.., cols c_i*bs..)；transposed-sparse metadata semantics: column_indices_t/offsets_t (CSR of A^T) and block_offsets_t (index into data of original block; block read with swapped strides)；consistency requirement between row_indices/offsets/column_indices and *_t metadata (not validated)；duplicate blocks: kernel accumulates both, to_dense keeps one；block size 128 only; pid_m used directly as block row (assumes BLOCK_M==block size)；dense-operand transpose inferred from strides | yes | grid=(M/128, N/128) with swizzle2d GROUP_M=128; loops over nnz blocks of block-row pid_m in K sub-tiles of 32. Used in backward (dense grads) of SDD/DDS. domain: M,K,N multiples of 128, unmasked loads, fp16/bf16 (Matrix validation insists on fp16 data but tests use bf16). deviation: fp32 via TF32 dot; same both-strides>1 transpose heuristic on rhs as sdd. |
| block_sparse_matmul_sdd (dense x dense -> BCSR-sparse output on given topology) | 前向+反向 | 需要定义 | K | BCSR topology format: data [nnz,bs,bs] row-major blocks, row_indices/column_indices int16 (one per block), offsets int32 CSR row pointers；which output blocks are produced (only topology blocks; entries outside topology undefined/absent)；duplicate or unsorted (row,col) entries: kernel writes each listed block independently; to_dense reference scatters (last wins)；block size fixed to 128 (BLOCK_M=BLOCK_N=BLOCK_SIZE=128)；transposition of dense operands inferred from strides；accumulate fp32 (int32 for non-float dtypes) and single rounding to output dtype | yes | grid=(nnz_blocks,); program pid reads (row_indices[pid], column_indices[pid]) and computes one 128x128 block of A@B over full K. Backward of DSD/DDS uses this kernel for the sparse-data gradient. domain: M,K,N must be multiples of 128/32/128 (asserted), no masks; rm%M wrap; out contiguous [nnz,128,128]. deviation: fp32 inputs go through tl.dot default (TF32 on sm80+); wrapper treats an operand as transposed only when both strides>1 and then swaps strides, which is wrong for general non-contiguous views (transposed views stride (1,K) are handled correctly without the flag). |

## trident

https://github.com/kakaobrain/trident，提交 `6c429c6b1a2d`，许可 Apache-2.0；`@triton.jit` 函数 73 个，入口 kernel 52 个。

| 分族 | 方向 | 类别 | 规格类别 | 参照程序，或待定事项 | sm_86 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| argmax | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.argmax(x, dim)  (x 2D) | yes | Ties -> first index (tl.argmax tie_break_left). NaN not propagated (torch treats NaN as max). Padding handled with -inf. |
| batch_norm backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(F.batch_norm(x, None, None, w, b, training=True, eps=eps), (x, w, b), g) | yes |  |
| batch_norm forward (always batch statistics) | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.batch_norm(x, running_mean, running_var, weight, bias, training=True, momentum=momentum, eps=eps)  (x [N,C,L]; running stats updated in place with unbiased var if given) | yes | DEVIATION: function.batch_norm ignores its `training` argument and the module passes track_running_stats there; eval mode (normalize with running stats) is never used and running stats are updated even in eval. Whole [N, L] per channel in one block (next_pow2 each). mean/var saved in input dtype. momentum=None unsupported. |
| cosine_similarity backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(F.cosine_similarity(x1, x2, dim, eps), (x1, x2), g) | yes | BUG: inside the loop over the reduced dim, \|\|x1\|\| and \|\|x2\|\| are recomputed from the current block only (tl.sum over block), so gradients are wrong whenever size_along_dim > autotuned block_size (256..2048). Divides by unclamped denominator (inf/NaN for zero vectors). |
| cosine_similarity forward | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.cosine_similarity(x1, x2, dim, eps)  [kernel formula: (x1*x2).sum(dim) / clamp(x1.norm(dim=dim)*x2.norm(dim=dim), min=eps)] | yes | Clamps the product of norms (older torch formula); current torch clamps each norm separately - differs only when a norm < eps. Inputs same shape, contiguous, rank<=3, no broadcasting. Also writes numerator/denominator for backward. |
| gelu backward (tanh approximation) | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(F.gelu(x, approximate='tanh'), x, g) | yes | Same ambiguity as forward. |
| gelu forward (tanh approximation) | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.gelu(x, approximate='tanh') | yes | Code implements the tanh approximation although trident.GELU docstring says plain GELU and tests compare to exact F.gelu(x) with atol=1e-2 (max diff ~5e-4). Intended reference ambiguous: 'tanh' (code) vs 'none' (nn.GELU default/tests). Uses tl.math.tanh (Triton 2.x API). |
| group_norm backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(F.group_norm(x, G, w, b, eps), (x, w, b), g) | yes | grad_output/input loaded with boundary_check but no padding_option (undefined padding); dW/db staging sums include padded lanes along L, correct only if padding reads as 0. Staging [N, C] summed in torch. |
| group_norm forward | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.group_norm(x, num_groups, weight, bias, eps) | yes | Input viewed [N, C, -1]; a whole group (C/G x L, each padded to pow2) in one block, so large groups may not compile. mean/rstd stored in input dtype. |
| instance_norm backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(F.instance_norm(x, rm, rv, w, b, use_input_stats, m, eps), (x, w, b), g) | yes | DEVIATION: with use_input_stats=0 the kernel still applies the batch-statistics gradient formula (subtracting mean/var terms) although stats are constants; correct grad would be w*rsqrt(rv+eps)*g. |
| instance_norm forward (+ running-stat update kernel) | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.instance_norm(x, running_mean, running_var, weight, bias, use_input_stats=use_input_stats, momentum=momentum, eps=eps)  (x [N,C,L]) | yes | Running update = (1-m)*r + m*mean_over_batch(per-instance mean / unbiased var), same as torch. Module deviation: passes use_input_stats = not track_running_stats and ignores self.training, so with track_running_stats=True it always normalizes with running stats and never updates them. mean/var stored in input dtype; momentum is a constexpr. |
| layer_norm backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(F.layer_norm(x, ns, w, b, eps), (x, w), g) | yes | BUG (suspected): with padding (x_size not pow2) centered_mean is not masked, so padded lanes contribute 2*(-mean)*grad_var to grad_mean -> grad_input off by a per-row constant when mean != 0 (tests use randn rows, mean~0). dW via per-row staging summed in torch; bias grad computed in torch as grad_output.sum(0) (wrong shape for >2D inputs). |
| layer_norm forward | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.layer_norm(x, normalized_shape, weight, bias, eps)  (+ mean, rstd saved) | yes | Whole normalized extent in one block (next_pow2). mean/rstd stored in input dtype (fp16 precision for backward). |
| leaky_relu backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(F.leaky_relu(x, s), x, g) | yes | grad at x=0 is slope (same as torch). |
| leaky_relu forward | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.leaky_relu(x, negative_slope) | yes |  |
| linear backward (dX, per-batch dW, per-batch db) | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(F.linear(x, W, b), (x, W, b), g) | yes | dW/db written per batch into staging [B, N, K]/[B, N] and summed in torch; low-precision accumulation as forward. Also used by GEGLU backward. |
| linear forward | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.linear(x, W, b)  (x [B,M,K] or 2D, W [N,K]) | yes | Accumulator dtype = input dtype: fp16 inputs accumulate in fp16 (tl.dot out_dtype=fp16); bf16 uses out_dtype fp16 then casts (range overflow >65504). use_accelerator=True enables TF32 for fp32. |
| max along dim backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(torch.max(x, dim).values, x, g) | yes | Scatters g to the saved argmax index; shapes are constexpr (recompile per shape). |
| max along dim forward (values, indices) | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.max(x, dim)  -> (values, indices)  (x 2D) | yes | BUG: padded lanes (x_size not pow2) loaded with undefined padding and not masked; if padding reads 0 and all row values are negative, argmax lands on a padded index and the value is read out of row bounds. NaN handling differs from torch. |
| mean along dim backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | (g / x.shape[dim]).unsqueeze(dim).expand_as(x) | yes |  |
| mean along dim forward | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.mean(x, dim)  (x 2D) | yes | Accumulates in input dtype; dim=None behaves as last dim. |
| prelu backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(F.prelu(x, w), (x, w), g) | yes | Kernel writes grad_input and a per-element weight-grad staging tensor g*min(x,0) shaped like input; wrapper sums only dim 2 -> grad_weight shape [N, C] instead of [C] for N>1 (wrapper bug, check staging tensor instead). |
| prelu forward (per-channel weight) | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.prelu(x, w) with w.numel() == x.shape[1] (C) | yes | Input viewed [N, C, -1]; weight indexed per channel with bounds of C, so num_parameters=1 (module default) with C>1 reads out of bounds instead of broadcasting (torch broadcasts). |
| relu backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(F.relu(x), x, g) | yes | relu'(0)=0 as torch. |
| relu forward | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.relu(x) | yes | Flat contiguous elementwise. All trident kernels target Triton ~2.1 (setup.py pins triton@main of 2023); several use APIs removed in Triton 3.x (tl.math.fast_expf/tanh/pow/max, tl.view) - porting needed for triton 3.6. |
| scaled dot-product attention backward (dropout_p=0) | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q,k,v,attn_mask=mask,is_causal=c,scale=1/sqrt(D)), (q,k,v,mask), do) | yes | delta = rowsum(o*do) via Softmax.backward_delta; recomputes P from forward log2sum. dq accumulated read-modify-write into caller-zeroed buffer. Fixed block 64 with no boundary checks -> requires N >= 64 (pow2 N in {16,32} reads OOB). grad_mask = dS (grad w.r.t. additive mask). |
| scaled dot-product attention forward (dropout_p=0) | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, attn_mask=float_mask, is_causal=is_causal, scale=1/sqrt(D))  (q,k,v [B,H,N,D]); aux log2sum = rowwise max + log2(sum) of scaled scores in base-2 | yes | Constraints: N and D powers of 2, D in {16,32,64,128}, q and k/v same N (y_size used for both), contiguous [B,H,N,D] (head offset = flat head index * stride(1)), float additive mask [B,H,N,N] (bool rejected), mask and is_causal mutually exclusive. No boundary checks: N must be a multiple of the autotuned block (16/32/64). Scores and output accumulate in input dtype (fp16 accumulation for fp16). log2sum stored in input dtype. |
| silu backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(F.silu(x), x, g) | yes |  |
| silu forward | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | F.silu(x) | yes | sigmoid via tl.math.fast_expf (approximate exp). |
| softmax backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | delta = (g*y).sum(dim); grad = y*(g - delta) == torch.autograd.grad(torch.softmax(x,dim), x, g) | yes | Takes saved output y. delta accumulated in input dtype (fp16 accumulation for fp16). |
| softmax forward (2D, online) | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.softmax(x, dim)  (x 2D, dim in {0,1}) | yes | Online max/sum per lane with tl.math.fast_expf. Edge deviation: when x_size % x_block_size != 0 and x_size > x_block_size, lanes padded in the last block rescale their partial sum by exp(max-0), overflowing (inf) for logits >~88 or underflowing (dropped mass) for logits <~-88. |
| sum along dim backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | g.unsqueeze(dim).expand_as(x) | yes |  |
| sum along dim forward | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.sum(x, dim)  (x 2D, dim in {0,1}) | yes | Accumulates in input dtype (fp16 accumulation for fp16). function.sum(dim=None) reduces the last dim of a 2D tensor (torch: full reduction). |
| var along dim backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(torch.var(x, dim, correction=c), x, g) | yes | Recomputes the mean (input-dtype accumulation). |
| var along dim forward | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.var(x, dim, correction=correction)  (x 2D) | yes | Per-lane Welford then tl.reduce with combine_welford (masked lanes use count += 2.2e-16). correction is constexpr; x_size == correction divides by zero (torch gives inf/nan similarly). |
| var_mean along dim backward | 反向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.autograd.grad(torch.var_mean(x, dim, correction=c), x, (g_var, g_mean)) | yes | DEVIATION: only g_var is used; the wrapper discards the gradient of the mean output (missing + g_mean/N term). |
| var_mean along dim forward | 前向 | torch 运算 |  | torch.var_mean(x, dim, correction=correction)  (x 2D) | yes |  |
| geglu backward | 反向 | torch 组合 |  | torch.autograd.grad(geglu_ref(x, W, b), (x, W, b), g) | yes | GEGLU.backward produces grad of h from saved h (whole row of N/2 per program); then Linear backward kernels. ctx.use_accelerator forced False. |
| geglu forward (fused linear + tanh-GELU gate) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | h = F.linear(x, W, b); s, g = h.chunk(2, -1); s * F.gelu(g, approximate='tanh')  (also stores h) | yes | Conventions from code: first half = value, second half = gate, tanh GELU (paper uses exact GELU on the gate). BUG: boundary heuristics inverted (require_*_boundary_check = size % block == 0), so when K and M (or K and N/2) are not multiples of the block sizes loads run unchecked out of bounds. fp16 accumulation as Linear. |
| masked softmax backward | 反向 | torch 组合 |  | torch.autograd.grad(forward_ref(x, mask), x, g) | yes | No grad for mask. |
| masked softmax forward | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | torch.softmax(x.masked_fill(mask > 2.220446049250313e-16, float('-inf')), dim)  (x 2D) | yes | Mask convention fixed by code only: a mask value > eps EXCLUDES the position (true/1 = masked out); mask must share x's shape and strides. Fully masked row -> NaN. Whole row in one block (next_pow2). |
| rms_norm backward | 反向 | torch 组合 |  | torch.autograd.grad(forward_ref(x, w, b), (x, w), g) | yes | Gradient through rms uses -x/(rms*rms + eps) instead of -x/(rms+eps)^2 (O(eps/rms) relative inconsistency with the forward). dW per-row staging summed in torch; bias grad in torch. |
| rms_norm forward (partial RMSNorm, eps outside sqrt) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | k = d if not 0<=p<=1 else int(d*p); rms = x[..., :k].pow(2).mean(-1, keepdim=True).sqrt(); x / (rms + eps) * w (+ b) | yes | Not torch.nn.functional.rms_norm: eps is added to rms outside the sqrt. Kernel passes partial_size = d*p as a float constexpr: sums lanes with index < d*p (i.e. ceil) but divides by the float d*p, differing from the paper's int(d*p) when d*p is non-integer; p=0 divides by zero. Weight required; functional eps default 1e-8, module 1e-5. Saves rms. |
| shift_gelu backward | 反向 | torch 组合 |  | torch.autograd.grad(F.gelu(x + b, approximate='tanh'), x, g) | yes | Consumes saved shift; bias grad = grad_input.sum(0) in torch. |
| shift_gelu forward (bias add + tanh-GELU) | 前向 | torch 组合 |  | F.gelu(x + b, approximate='tanh')  (x [M,N], b [N]); also stores shift = x + b | yes | Tests compare to exact F.gelu(x+b) at atol 1e-2; same tanh-vs-exact ambiguity as GELU. |
| attention with dropout (dropout_p>0, forward+backward) | 前向+反向 | 随机 | R | kernel: torch.where(m, sdpa_out/(1-p+eps), 0) with m = tl.rand(seed, arange(D) + y_block_offset) > p  (intended per signature: torch SDPA dropout on attention probabilities) | yes | DEVIATION: dropout applied to the normalized output, not to attention probabilities; mask indexed only by head-dim lane + row-block offset, so identical across rows of a block and across batch/heads, overlapping between blocks. Backward infers mask from output>0 and multiplies by (1-p+eps) instead of dividing. Deterministic check only conditional on the kernel's mask. Seed via torch.random.seed() (global RNG side effect). |
| dropout backward | 反向 | 随机 | R | torch.where(m, g/(1-p), 0) with the forward mask m | yes | DEVIATION/BUG: mask inferred as (p==0)\|(output>0) - kept elements with x<=0 get zero grad - and grad scaled by (1-p+eps) instead of 1/(1-p). Correct only for p in {0,1} (the only tested values). |
| dropout forward | 前向 | 随机 | R | torch.where(m, x/(1-p+2.22e-16), 0), m = tl.rand(seed, flat_offset) > p | yes | Deterministic check: conditional on mask (zeros of output where x!=0) kept values equal x/(1-p); fraction kept ~1-p. Seed = torch.random.seed(), which re-seeds torch's global RNG nondeterministically (side effect) and is a 64-bit value passed to a tl.int32-annotated arg. |

## OpenAI triton_kernels (python/triton_kernels in triton-lang/triton)

https://github.com/triton-lang/triton，提交 `e6f4c919fb18`，许可 MIT (repo-root LICENSE)；`@triton.jit` 函数 101 个，入口 kernel 31 个。

| 分族 | 方向 | 类别 | 规格类别 | 参照程序，或待定事项 | sm_86 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| bitmatrix column counts (expert histogram) + per-32-row partials | — | torch 组合 |  | bits=((B[:n_rows].view(torch.int32).long().unsqueeze(-1) >> torch.arange(32)) & 1).flatten(1); col_sum=bits.sum(0)[:n_cols]; partials[p]=bits[p*BLOCK_M:(p+1)*BLOCK_M].sum(0) | yes | Despite the name it returns per-column sums. n_rows may be a device scalar; out-of-range rows read as 0; int32 atomics (exact). |
| masked compaction of top-k (value,index) rows by bitmask | — | torch 组合 |  | keep=((bitmask.gather(1, yi.long()//32) >> (yi.long()%32)) & 1).bool(); order=(~keep).int().argsort(1, stable=True); out_v=torch.where(keep.gather(1,order), yv.gather(1,order), sentinel); out_i likewise  [= compaction.py::compaction_torch] | yes | domain: K = yi.shape[1] must be a power of two; yv/yi rows assumed contiguous (kernel ignores their strides); sentinel cast to yv dtype; indices < 32*bitmask.shape[1]. |
| matmul launch-metadata flops/bytes counter | — | torch 组合 |  | s=SliceSizes[:NUM_SLICES].long(); flops=STATIC_FLOPS + s.sum().double()*FLOPS_PER_TOKEN; bytes=STATIC_BYTES + s.sum()*(X_BYTES_PER_TOKEN+Y_BYTES_PER_TOKEN+W_BYTES_PER_TOKEN) + (s>0).sum()*W_BYTES_PER_ACTIVE_SLICE | yes | Profiling (proton launch_metadata) helper, grid (1,). domain: all per-token factors are constexpr; flops fp64, bytes int64. |
| Blackwell MX scale swizzle (weights and activations, batched or ragged) | 前向+反向 | 需要定义 | J | 128x4 tile -> (32,4,4) swizzle order；padding K to 4/8 and N to 128；ragged mode: per-slice 128-row blocks via block schedule, zero logical tail on inverse | yes | Byte permutation only; layout targets sm100 consumers. |
| Blackwell MX4 shuffled weight layout conversion | 前向+反向 | 需要定义 | J | tile shape (block_k/2 x block_n) order；padding to lcm(128, tile_k) bytes and tile_n；nibble transpose when source is N-packed | yes | Layout consumed only by the persistent sm100 matmul. |
| Blackwell MXFP4 value repack (K-packed <-> N-packed) | 前向+反向 | 需要定义 | J | fp4 nibble order when changing packing axis；storage padding of M to 128 rows；tile-shuffled source variant (BLOCK_SHAPE) | yes | Used for both swizzle and unswizzle; uint8 only. |
| FPSan embed/unembed (gluon) | 前向+反向 | 需要定义 | O | float -> same-width integer ring embedding defined by Triton's FPSan instrumentation；unembed inverse and unsigned payload handling；fp8 dtype support | unknown | Gluon kernel (BlockedLayout); debugging utility. Checkable invariants: unembed(embed(x)) bitwise == x; embed(1.0) == 1. |
| Hopper MX scale swizzle | 前向+反向 | 需要定义 | J | num_warps-dependent permutation；padding to 32*num_warps rows and even K, pad value 0 | yes | INVERSE flag selects swizzle/unswizzle; uint8 permutation only. |
| Hopper MXFP4 value swizzle (MMA tile permutation + fp4 bit interleave) | 前向+反向 | 需要定义 | J | MMA v2/v3 tile permutation；4-fp4 bit interleave encoding for fast bf16 dequant；padding alignment (64/256) and pad value；mx_axis and source packing axis | yes | Pure integer byte permutation; exact inverse pair (roundtrip checkable). Layout consumed by Hopper matmul paths. |
| SwiGLU (GPT-OSS variant, interleaved gate/linear, clamp limit, flexpoint) | 前向 | 需要定义 | O | interleaved layout: even columns = gate, odd = linear；asymmetric clamp (gate upper only, linear both sides)；'+1' on the linear branch；flexpoint: input scale applied before clamp, output divided by expected scale, actual_scale absmax atomics, saturate_inf；routing_data n_tokens limits rows (rest unwritten) | yes | domain: last dim even and contiguous; compute fp32; out dtype = a.dtype. deviation: sigmoid via exp2_ftz (flushes denormals); swiglu_torch has no flex support. Plain (no-flex) mode is fully fixed by swiglu_torch. |
| bitmatrix metadata (col-sorted / row-sorted nonzero indices) | — | 需要定义 | J | stable order within each column (by row-major flat index)；-1 sentinel padding of col_sorted_indx/row_sorted_indx；precondition: bitmatrix and nonzero_indx encode the same sets (counts come from the bitmap, positions from the indices)；limits: column index < 65535, per-block entries <= 32768 | yes | Stage 1 = exclusive scans of partial column sums (in place), col_offs, memset to -1; stage 2 = block-local stable sort + keyed scan. MoE gather/scatter index construction. |
| dequantize from MX (mxfp4/mxfp8 values, E8M0 or E4M3 scales) | 前向 | 需要定义 | F | E8M0 decode: 0x00 -> 2^-127 (subnormal), 0xFF -> NaN；overflow saturates to dst max finite instead of Inf；Inf/NaN in fp8e5m2 values；E4M3 (NVFP4) scale decode; block size inferred from scale dtype (uint8->32, e4m3->16)；tensor_scale post-multiply (torch wrapper) | yes | Host TensorDescriptor is rewritten to pointer loads below sm90 (nvidia compiler make_ttir), so mxfp4/e5m2 paths run on sm86; e4m3 needs sm89+. deviation: kernel clamps after scaling, so e5m2 +-Inf come out as +-max finite while upcast_from_mxfp_torch keeps Inf (TODO in kernel); NaN through tl.clamp. |
| expert-parallel token dispatch/combine over symmetric memory | 前向+反向 | 需要定义 | H | expert->rank ownership bitmask (expert owned by no rank maps to rank 0 because rank = sum(r*owned))；destination row indexing via gate_indx/topk_indx (flat positions) and token partition n_tokens_local*rank；rows never written in destination buffers；duplicate destinations and peer write ordering (barrier after launch) | yes | Plain loads/stores through peer pointers; single-rank direct launch runs on one GPU (test_convert_large_strides); multi-rank needs torch symmetric memory/P2P. Single-rank dp_to_ep = dst[gate_indx[m,j]] = src[m] (scatter copy). |
| masked/scaled reduction over one axis (split-K reducer, MoE combine) | 前向 | 需要定义 | H | MX input: E8M0 scale decode along last dim in groups of 32；MX/NVFP4 output quantization (scale rounding, e2m1 RTNE, nibble packing, value pack factor)；flexpoint: x scale, x_global_scale, out expected scale, actual_scale = absmax/max_finite+1e-30 via atomic_max, xor checksum, saturate_inf；postprocess_fn1 (grouped reduction_n, e.g. swiglu) / postprocess_fn2 closures；unpadded_batch_size: rows past it are not written；summation order (bitmap path sums active inputs in ascending k) | yes | domain: x must be 3D; mask/scale broadcast via stride 0; compute dtype fp32 (fp64 when x is fp64); masked-out entries contribute 0. deviation: reduce_torch ignores unpadded_batch_size and x_global_scale; kernel leaves rows >= unpadded uninitialized (torch.empty). mxfp8 output path needs fp8e4nv (sm89+). |
| matmul_ogs MoE GEMM, non-persistent (gather/scatter/ragged/MX/flex) | 前向 | 需要定义 | H | gather_indx row mapping (no -1 handling) and scatter_indx (-1 = drop, overwrite not accumulate, collisions undefined)；ragged metadata format for ragged-M (MoE) and ragged-K (dW = X^T dY per slice) modes, incl. padded/unused rows；MX operand formats: mxfp4/mxfp8 values, E8M0 vs E4M3 (NVFP4) block scales, block size 32/16, per-row tensor scales, NVFP4->MXFP8 requantization (sm100)；swizzled value/scale layouts (HOPPER_VALUE, HOPPER/BLACKWELL/CDNA4/CDNA5 scales)；flexpoint per-tensor/per-batch scales: expected/actual/checksum semantics, saturate_inf；epilogue order: acc*xs*ws + bias*beta, *out_alpha, fused activation (reduction_n), *gamma, + c_acc_in*acc_scale, then flex/MX output quantization；accumulation precision: fp32 acc (fp64 only if both fp64), TF32 rounding of fp32 inputs when allow_tf32 (default True), max_num_imprecise_acc；split-K partials reduced by reduce() with activation moved there；fused multi-GPU output comm (map_dst_coord, n_reduce_shards) | yes | domain: wrapper picks _p_matmul only if is_tma_compliant(), which is False below sm90, so on sm86 every call runs _matmul; dense bf16/fp16/fp32/fp64 path is plain tl.dot. fp32 inputs are rounded to TF32 by default (allow_tf32=True). Gathered row offsets wrap modulo slice size (offs % eM) and are masked on store. Bias is added only by split k=0; Betas scale bias only; Gammas are applied after the activation. deviation: matmul_torch dequantizes MX operands with the Triton kernel upcast_from_mxfp, so the library reference is not independent of the library's Triton code; it also ignores out_alpha, fused activation/epilogue, c_acc_in, tensor scales and fused comm, and zero-fills rows that the kernel leaves unwritten (ragged-M padding rows, dropped scatter rows); docstring writes Y[idx] += but kernel overwrites. MX paths use tl.dot_scaled (compiler-decomposed below sm100; per audit rule treat as not-sm86); fp8e4nv operands need sm89+. |
| matmul_ogs MoE GEMM, persistent TMA | 前向 | 需要定义 | H | same as non-persistent matmul_ogs；TMA gather/scatter (sm100) and shuffled MXFP4 weight layout (BLACKWELL_MX4_VALUE_SHUFFLED)；tiled operands (ragged-K only)；CLC scheduling and epilogue subtiling | no | Uses tensor descriptors (TMA), descriptor gather/scatter, warp_specialize loops, tl.dot_scaled; selected only when is_tma_compliant() (sm90+). Persistent path disables fp fusion (enable_fp_fusion=False) so its rounding differs from _matmul. deviation: same reference caveats as _matmul. |
| quantize to MX (mxfp4/mxfp8 with E8M0 scales; NVFP4 with E4M3 scales) | 前向 | 需要定义 | F | scale rounding: default ROUND_UP 2^ceil(log2(amax/max_q)) vs OCP ROUND_DOWN floor(log2(amax/max_pow2_q)); ROUND_NEAREST for E4M3 scales；all-zero block: scale byte 0 and quant scale 0；e2m1 RTNE with saturation to +-6 and subnormal handling；fp8 element conversion satfinite；nibble packing (even element in low nibble)；NaN/Inf inputs in block max；NVFP4 block 16 E4M3 scale without global scale；padding exclusion from amax | yes | sm86 covers mxfp4 + E8M0 (bit-manipulation e2m1 encoder used below sm100) and e5m2; e4m3 values or E4M3 scales need sm89+. domain: quant axis divisible by microblock size; output value stride 1 on quant axis; src bf16/fp16/fp32. |
| ragged segment pad/unpad to block-aligned rows | 前向+反向 | 需要定义 | H | ragged metadata format (block schedule at block size ALIGN_M)；pad value 0; rows beyond sum(slice_sizes) zero (wrapper zero-fills)；column padding to K_pad | yes | Semi-persistent loops over valid blocks only; pad and unpad are inverses on valid rows. |
| ragged tensor metadata (slice/block offsets, packed block schedule) | — | 需要定义 | J | block-size set (16..128 on CUDA, +256 on HIP)；schedule entry packing (block<<16)\|slice in int32, -1 padding；max_n_blocks bound / truncation；exclusive cumsums with total in the last slot | yes | domain: requires sum(slice_sizes) <= n_total_rows; otherwise the compute kernel writes schedule entries past max_n_blocks (no bound check) where the torch ref truncates. slice ids < 65536. |
| ragged tensor metadata remap (drop/renumber slices) | — | 需要定义 | J | slice_map semantics (-1 = drop, else new slice id)；order-preserving compaction with -1 sentinels；trailing block_offs slot = number of kept blocks | yes | Single CTA per block size; schedule entries with slice id 0xFFFF treated as empty. |
| reduce backward | 反向 | 需要定义 | H | gradient through postprocess_fn1 is taken as a grouped-sum broadcast regardless of the actual fn；x_global_scale and output flex scales are ignored；no gradient for MX-quantized outputs (raises)；rows >= unpadded_batch_size left unwritten | yes | Inherits forward conventions. deviation: dx allocated with torch.empty, so skipped rows are garbage. |
| reduce row scheduler (active-input counts + heavy-first row order) | — | 需要定义 | J | exact RowIdxs permutation depends on BLOCK_M/BLOCK_S0/grid (per-CTA strided row sets reordered as >=4,3,2,<=1 active inputs, stable) vs only requiring a valid per-CTA permutation；rows beyond UnpaddedBatchSize are not written | yes | Internal helper for the _reduce_forward bitmap path (per-row mask, stride_m1==0, K<=32). domain: mask loaded with K <= BLOCK_K. |
| top-k backward (softmax gradient scattered to selected experts) | 反向 | 需要定义 | H | inherits index set/order from forward；duplicate indices in user-provided y_indx (last write wins)；rows >= n_rows not written | yes | deviation: dx = torch.empty_like(x), so rows >= n_rows are uninitialized; softmax recomputed in fp32. |
| top-k routing (sorted top-k + optional softmax + bitmatrix) | 前向 | 需要定义 | H | tie-breaking: smaller expert index wins；total order on float bit patterns (-0.0 < +0.0, +NaN largest, -NaN smallest) vs torch.topk；outputs sorted by value descending；softmax over the k selected logits in fp32, cast to x.dtype；index dtype int16；bitmatrix format: uint32 words, bit j of word w = expert 32w+j, transposed storage with rows padded to 32；provided y_indx mode: gather at given indices without re-sorting；rows >= n_rows left untouched；all_gather to symmetric-memory peers | yes | domain: 2D input, dim=1, n_cols < 32768, k <= n_cols; padded columns masked to key 0. deviation: topk_torch ranks with argsort(-x, stable), so +0/-0 ties and NaN ordering differ from the kernel's bit-key order; topk_torch fills rows >= n_rows (x[n_rows:, :k], index -1) while the kernel leaves them uninitialized. |
| random block signs (Philox) | — | 随机 | R | Philox counter layout (offset//4 + block, lane) and host offset advance (4*n_blocks) | yes | Test-data helper. Deterministic checks: values in {0,1}; zero where lane >= min/max valid extent (random_count); identical output for identical (seed, offset); bitwise equal to philox(seed, counter_lo, counter_hi, lane, 0)[0] & 1 with a Philox4x32-10 implementation. |
